Method: 通过分析高斯核的解析性及其谱密度指数衰减特性，论证其对条件方差和数值稳定性的负面影响。

Result: 高斯核导致不切实际的小条件方差（引发灾难性过度自信）及数值病态（需nugget项修正），问题根源在于核的解析性而非高斯形式本身。

Conclusion: 应避免使用解析核（包括高斯核），因其指数衰减的谱密度特性导致模型脆弱，需寻找更稳健的替代核函数。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [216] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于表示的测量应对于保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性，但列置换并行分析违反了这一原则，其结果可能反映隐藏坐标的选择而非模型的固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏坐标并非由语言模型的输入输出函数唯一确定，因此从表示中导出的测量应当对保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性。然而，现有的列置换并行分析方法可能违反了这一不变性原则，导致测量结果不稳定。

Method: 通过理论分析和实证研究，评估列置换并行分析在保持函数不变重参数化下的表现。研究涉及五个模型、三个检索领域和75种变换，并对比了仅中心化控制与独立并行分析种子的效果。

Result: 在75种变换中，中位数组件计数不一致率为0.79，中位数固定阈值决策不一致率为0.26。在仅中心化控制下，1200个组件计数中有1141个发生变化，而独立并行分析种子未改变相应决策。正交不变比较器分数在数值上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 并行分析导出的组件计数和决策可能反映隐藏坐标的选择，而非模型的明确定义属性。正交不变比较器分数在数值上更稳定，更适合用于表示测量。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [217] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动扩散的逆问题求解器，通过迭代检测和纠正模型误差，即使在初始训练边界不包含真实参数的情况下，也能发现并学习正确的参数空间区域，适用于科学和工程中的逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程应用中常需从实验数据中估计未知参数，这是一个因非线性、噪声和不适定性而极具挑战的逆问题，现有方法在初始先验知识不完整时难以保证鲁棒推断。

Method: 训练扩散模型（DM）学习参数空间与可观测空间之间的映射，通过后验不确定性迭代检测和纠正模型误差，实现自适应域增强，基于贝叶斯原理确保在不完全先验知识下的鲁棒推断。

Result: 在具有无限解的玩具逆问题和量子色动力学分析中核子结构的事件可观测量量子关联函数参数化两个案例中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为不完全先验知识下的逆问题提供了原则性的贝叶斯 justification，实现了自适应域增强和鲁棒推断，在科学和工程应用中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [218] [Recovering Expert Critic-Sourced Network Adjacency between Musical Artists from Acoustic Distributions: A Construct-Validity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27291)
*Elena Badillo-Goicoechea,Fengfeng He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出利用乐评人建立的“关键邻近性”（critical adjacency）作为音乐推荐的第三信号，通过声学特征验证了其有效性。研究发现，在冷启动场景下，基于声学内容的模型能以0.767的AUC复现乐评人关联，且共识度越高复现率越高（最高0.865），证明该信号包含可复现的“声学核心”和受社会语境影响的“社会学余项”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐推荐主要依赖用户 - 物品交互（冷启动失效）和内在音乐内容。乐评人通过长文显式链接艺术家形成的“关键邻近性”蕴含了更丰富、更有原则的判断，但缺乏外部验证来确定这种关系是基于音乐本身还是社会语境。

Method: 将艺术家表示为80种低层Essentia声学描述符的经验分布，利用边缘最优传输（Wasserstein距离）建模成对邻近性。在冷启动、艺术家不相交的分割下，评估关键邻近性的声学可复现性，并进行分层评估以对比不同流派的社会学模型。

Result: 集成模型在样本外AUC达到0.767（95% CI 0.761-0.775）。可复现性随关键共识度单调上升，在多源证实的边上达到0.865。分层评估显示，基于场景的紧密流派比广泛的行业术语具有更高的可复现性。

Conclusion: 关键话语是推荐的丰富信息源，可分解为可复现的“声学核心”和由叙事定位、亚文化语境及经典地位驱动的“社会学余项”。该工作提供了可扩展的冷启动发现机制，并为音乐信息检索（MIR）和推荐系统（MRS）研究提供了社会学基础方法。

Abstract: Music recommendation relies primarily on two signals: user-item interactions, which fail in the cold-start regime, and intrinsic musical content, available for any recording. We argue that a third, largely untapped signal is both richer and more principled: critical adjacency, the pairwise relation established when an expert critic explicitly links two artists in long-form prose. It encodes deliberate judgments about which artists belong together. Prior work established its internal validity, showing it recovers coherent, interpretable communities and can match collaborative filtering in user-satisfaction simulations, with no user data. What has been missing is external validation: whether this critic-sourced relation is grounded in the music itself versus sociological context. We test it against acoustic content, reframing the question as one of construct validity.
  Representing artists as empirical distributions over 80 low-level Essentia acoustic descriptors and modeling pairwise proximity via marginal optimal-transport (Wasserstein) distances, we evaluate how far critical adjacency is sonically recoverable under a cold-start, artist-disjoint split. Our ensemble recovers these edges at out-of-sample AUC of 0.767 (95% CI 0.761-0.775). Recoverability rises monotonically with critical consensus, reaching 0.865 on multi-source attested edges. Stratified evaluations align with sociological models of genre: tightly bounded, scene-based genres show higher recoverability than broad industry umbrella terms.
  Critical discourse is thus a rich source of information for recommendation, decomposing into a reproducible "sonic core" and a "sociological remainder" driven by narrative positioning, subcultural context, and canonical placement. The work offers both a scalable cold-start discovery mechanism and a sociologically grounded approach to MIR and MRS research.

</details>


### [219] [A Finite Sample Analysis for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27313)
*Zijie Cheng,Xiang Li,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了表格型分布强化学习中同步分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的全局有限样本保证，区分了局部随机波动与全局样本复杂度，证明了在特定步长下，最终迭代波动阶数为 $\widetilde O(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-\gamma})$ 且与分位数数量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决分布强化学习中QTD算法缺乏全局有限样本理论保证的问题，特别是需要厘清算法收敛过程中全局收敛性与局部随机波动之间的区别，以及量化分位数数量对收敛速率的影响。

Method: 证明过程分离了两种稳定性机制：首先利用奖励累积分布函数的序单调性和分布贝尔曼算子的 $W_\infty$ 压缩性，通过全局比较论证将任意初始迭代带入局部邻域；其次在该邻域内线性化QTD平均场，利用其雅可比矩阵为非奇异M-矩阵及关联正半群的性质，进行方差敏感的鞅分析。

Result: 对于步长 $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ ($a\in(1/2,1)$)，主导的最终迭代波动阶数为 $\widetilde O(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-\gamma})$，且不依赖于分位数数量的多项式项；然而，确定性瞬态和所需的预热时间仍依赖于最小的贝尔曼目标密度（最坏情况下为 $m^{-1}$ 阶）。

Conclusion: 该结果清晰地界定了局部随机波动与全局样本复杂度之间的差异，表明QTD算法的随机波动性能优异（与分位数数量无关），但全局收敛的瞬态过程仍受限于目标分布的密度特性。

Abstract: We establish a global finite-sample guarantee for synchronous quantile temporal-difference learning (QTD) in tabular distributional reinforcement learning. The proof separates two stability mechanisms. A global comparison argument, based on the order monotonicity of reward cumulative distribution functions and the $W_\infty$ contraction of the distributional Bellman operator, brings an arbitrarily initialized iterate into a local neighborhood. Inside that neighborhood, we linearize the QTD mean field. Its Jacobian is a nonsingular $M$-matrix, and the associated positive semigroup permits a variance-sensitive martingale analysis. For stepsizes $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ with $a\in(1/2,1)$, the leading last-iterate fluctuation is of order $\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$ and has no polynomial dependence on the number of quantiles. The deterministic transient and the required burn-in can still depend on the smallest Bellman-target density, which is of order $m^{-1}$ in the worst case. The result therefore distinguishes sharply between the local stochastic fluctuation and the global sample complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Weight Distributions of Single Parity-Check Product Codes via Character Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26457)
*Makson Miller Alves Ribeiro,Sara D. Cardell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了二进制单奇偶校验乘积码的结构和枚举性质，确定了其广义汉明重量层次、最大码字重量，并推导了重量枚举器的精确闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单奇偶校验乘积码在编码理论中具有重要地位，但其广义汉明重量层次和重量分布的精确计算仍缺乏系统研究，需要建立有效的数学工具进行分析。

Method: 通过表征对偶码，应用 Walsh-Hadamard 形式的 MacWilliams 恒等式推导重量枚举器闭式表达式；利用 Krawtchouk 多项式建立无需穷举的完整重量分布计算程序。

Result: 确定了乘积码的广义汉明重量层次，证明了方阵乘积码的齐次重量枚举器对称性当且仅当 n 为偶数，获得了用二项式系数和交替卷积表示的系数显式公式。

Conclusion: 所提出的数学方法能够精确计算单奇偶校验乘积码的重量分布，避免了穷举枚举，数值示例验证了公式的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate structural and enumerative properties of binary single parity-check product codes. For each $n\geq 2$, $\operatorname{SPC}(n)$ denotes the binary single parity-check code of length $n$, consisting of all binary vectors of length $n$ having even Hamming weight. We determine the generalized Hamming weight hierarchy of the product code $\mathcal{C}_{m,n}=\operatorname{SPC}(m)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, whose codewords can be represented as $m\times n$ binary matrices in which every row and every column has even Hamming weight. For the square product $\mathcal{C}_n =\operatorname{SPC}(n)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, we also determine the maximum codeword weight and prove that its homogeneous weight enumerator is symmetric if and only if $n$ is even. After characterizing the dual code, we apply the MacWilliams identity in its Walsh--Hadamard formulation to derive an exact closed-form expression for the weight enumerator. By grouping the auxiliary binary vectors according to their Hamming weights, we obtain an explicit formula for each coefficient in terms of binomial coefficients and alternating convolutions. Finally, using Krawtchouk polynomials, we present an exact procedure for computing the full weight distribution without exhaustively enumerating all codewords. Numerical examples illustrate the formulas and verify the resulting computations.

</details>


### [221] [Decoding Algorithms for MDS Array Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26483)
*Sara D. Cardell,Gustavo Terra Bastos,Cintya Wink de Oliveira Benedito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵克罗内克积构建的MDS阵列码的解码过程，针对擦除信道和q元对称信道分别提出了高效的解码算法，并探讨了超正则矩阵为范德蒙矩阵时的特殊代数结构优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用MDS阵列码校验矩阵的特殊结构，开发针对不同信道模型（擦除信道和q元对称信道）的高效解码算法，以解决符号擦除和符号错误的恢复问题。

Method: 通过分析校验矩阵结构，设计针对擦除信道的任意$n-k$个符号擦除恢复算法；针对q元对称信道，推导单个和双个符号错误的定位与数值求解显式过程；特别利用范德蒙矩阵的代数特性优化解码流程。

Result: 成功构建了能恢复任意$n-k$个符号擦除的算法，给出了q元对称信道下单双符号错误的显式解码步骤，并通过不同有限域上的实例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用MDS阵列码的结构特性及范德蒙矩阵的代数优势，可显著提升其在不同信道模型下的解码效率和错误恢复能力。

Abstract: We study decoding procedures for a family of MDS array codes previously constructed from the Kronecker product of a superregular matrix and a non-singular matrix over a finite field. By exploiting the particular structure of their parity-check matrices, we develop decoding algorithms for different channel models. For the erasure channel, we provide an algorithm capable of recovering any pattern of up to $n-k$ symbol erasures. For the $q$-ary symmetric channel, we investigate the decoding of one and two symbol errors and give explicit procedures for determining their locations and values. We also consider the particular case in which the superregular matrix is a Vandermonde matrix, showing how its additional algebraic structure can be exploited in the decoding process. Explicit examples over different finite fields are provided to illustrate the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [222] [Knowledge Distillation Driven Semantic NOMA with GAN Refinement for 6G Robotic Vehicle Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27198)
*Qifei Wang,Zhen Gao,Li Qiao,Ziwei Wan,De Mi,Dapeng Li,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出KDG-SemNOMA框架，通过知识蒸馏和生成模型增强NOMA，实现6G机器人车辆的高保真视觉感知通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G赋能的机器人车辆需要高保真视觉感知，但面临带宽和能量限制；语义通信虽频谱高效，但在上行NOMA网络中受严重干扰。

Method: 1. 开发基于ConvNeXt的DeepJSCC架构，增强注意力特征模块用于动态信道适应；2. 采用两阶段知识蒸馏策略，正交传输教师模型指导NOMA学生模型以减少干扰；3. 引入通道条件GAN，以Stage-I初始重建和信道状态为条件输入，优化像素级平滑伪影。

Result: 在FFHQ-256数据集上实验表明，KDG-SemNOMA在像素级精度和感知保真度方面显著优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了6G机器人车辆通信中的干扰和能效问题，实现了高保真视觉感知。

Abstract: To achieve sustainable intelligent mobility, 6G-empowered robotic vehicles (RVs) require high-fidelity visual perception under stringent bandwidth and energy constraints. Semantic communication offers a spectral-efficient solution but suffers from severe interference in uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) RV networks. To address this, we propose a knowledge distillation-driven and generative models-enhanced NOMA framework for robust and green RV communications, named KDG-SemNOMA. First, we develop a ConvNeXt-based deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) architecture with an enhanced attention feature (AF) module for dynamic channel adaptation. Second, to mitigate interference without inference overhead, an orthogonal transmission teacher model guides the NOMA student model via a two-stage knowledge distillation strategy. Finally, to address the over-smoothing artifacts of pixel-wise optimization, we introduce a channel-conditional GAN (cGAN). By explicitly taking the Stage-I initial reconstruction and channel states as conditional inputs, this module refines coarse outputs into high-fidelity images with realistic textures. Experiments on FFHQ-256 demonstrate that KDG-SemNOMA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pixel-level accuracy and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [223] [Sharp Minimax Regret for Infinite-Memory Logistic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26515)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有无限输入记忆的有限字母表、外生驱动源的在线预测问题，通过局部贝叶斯混合和Toeplitz设计逆定理，证明了滞后解析谱Γ_T(r)是该源类在典型机制下的最小最大累积遗憾尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有无限输入记忆的外生驱动源的在线预测问题，理解滞后对预测的影响以及累积遗憾的理论界限，特别是在指数和多项式包络下的表现。

Method: 采用局部贝叶斯混合方法证明上界，使用Toeplitz设计逆定理证明下界，并分析截断输入对遗憾的影响，最后通过缩放在线牛顿预测器达到谱上界。

Result: 证明了对于每个可和包络，累积遗憾R_T(r)≤CΓ_T(r)；在指数和多项式包络下，R_T(r)≥cΓ_T(r)，确定了Γ_T(r)为最小最大累积遗憾尺度，并给出了具体衰减速率下的遗憾阶数。

Conclusion: Γ_T(r)是该源类在典型机制下的最小最大累积遗憾尺度，截断最近h个输入的成本与最坏情况截断轮廓可能导致多项式不同的遗憾，缩放在线牛顿预测器可达到谱上界。

Abstract: We study online prediction for a specific finite-alphabet, exogenously driven source with infinite input memory. Independent Rademacher inputs $(U_t)$ are observed sequentially, and the next binary mark has logit $\sum_{j=1}^{t}θ_jU_{t+1-j}$, where $\abs{θ_j}\leq r_j$ and $\sum_jr_j\leq B$. Regret is expected cumulative excess log loss. Lag $j$ can affect prediction by scale $r_j$ and enters only $n_{T,j}=T-j+1$ prediction rounds, leading to the lag-resolved spectrum $Γ_T(r)=\sum_{j=1}^{T}\log\!\left(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2\right)$. For every summable envelope, a localized Bayesian mixture proves $\cR_T(r)\leq CΓ_T(r)$. For exponential and polynomial envelopes, under the stated finite-sample dimension condition, a Toeplitz-design converse proves $\cR_T(r)\geq cΓ_T(r)$, with constants allowed to depend on the fixed decay parameters and the logit bound. Thus $Γ_T(r)$ is the minimax cumulative-regret scale for this source class in these canonical regimes, giving $Θ(α^{-1}\log^2T)$ for $r_j=Ae^{-αj}$ and $Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$ for $r_j=Aj^{-s}$, $s>1$. The converse is specific to the exogenous lagged model and is not a profile-only theorem for arbitrary stationary infinite-memory sources. Retaining only the most recent $h$ inputs costs order $\sum_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j^2$, yet the same worst-case truncation profile can correspond to polynomially different regret. A scaled online Newton predictor attains the spectrum upper bound.

</details>


### [224] [Spectral Approximation and Ergodic-Capacity Convergence of HMIMO Channels under Spatial-Wavenumber Domain Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26802)
*Hangsong Yan,Hong Yang,Shu Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对具有方形孔径和圆形波数支持的连续全息多输入多输出（HMIMO）信道，建立了有限维谱近似和遍历容量收敛的定量结果，证明了基于张量积子空间的投影方法能保持超指数误差衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续 HMIMO 信道中空间 - 波数域失配导致不可分的方 - 盘集中问题，使得基于球状长球面波函数（PSWF）的经典可分构造无法直接应用，需要解决非对角耦合下的谱近似和容量收敛问题。

Method: 将连续算子投影到一维 PSWF 的张量积子空间上，同时保持圆形波数支持，生成高度稀疏的有限维矩阵；通过一维 PSWF 特征值尾部包络控制全谱近似误差，并推导遍历容量差距的非渐近上界。

Result: 全谱近似误差在显式一维截断阈值后呈超指数衰减；特征谱在扁平化二维排序下获得渐近上包络；遍历容量差距界限继承相同的超指数依赖性；数值结果表明传统截断可能遗漏性能相关模式。

Conclusion: 提出的张量 -PSWF 截断方法有效解决了不可分集中问题，为连续 HMIMO 信道的有限维近似提供了理论保证，且优于传统的空间自由度截断方法。

Abstract: We establish quantitative results on finite-dimensional spectral approximation and ergodic-capacity convergence for continuous Holographic Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (HMIMO) channels with square apertures and physically prescribed circular wavenumber support. The resulting spatial-wavenumber domain mismatch leads to a non-separable square-disk concentration problem for which the classical separable construction based on prolate spheroidal wave functions (PSWFs) cannot be directly applied. We project the continuous operator onto a tensor-product subspace of one-dimensional (1D) PSWFs while preserving the circular wavenumber support, yielding a generally non-diagonal but highly sparse finite-dimensional matrix. We show that the whole-spectrum approximation error, accounting for retained-eigenvalue perturbations and the residual spectral tail, remains controlled by a 1D PSWF eigenvalue-tail envelope despite the loss of separability and induced off-diagonal coupling. Beyond an explicit 1D truncation threshold, this error decays super-exponentially. This analysis further yields an asymptotic upper envelope for the eigenspectrum under the flattened two-dimensional eigenvalue ordering. We further establish a non-asymptotic upper bound on the gap between the actual ergodic capacities of the continuous and tensor-PSWF-truncated channels under their respective transmit-covariance optimizations. Combined with the spectral result, this capacity-gap bound inherits the same super-exponential dependence on the truncation order. Finally, quadrature rules with explicit radial and angular node thresholds are developed for evaluating the projected matrix. Numerical results show that conventional truncation based on spatial degrees of freedom can omit performance-relevant modes, particularly for compact apertures.

</details>


### [225] [Minimum Rate For Partially Observable Linear System with Side Information: LQG Plant and Gaussian-Markov Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26917)
*Sijie Li,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有边信息的部分可观测线性系统所需的最小速率，证明了线性策略足以优化条件定向信息下界，并表明在标量情况下优化问题是凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注全观测或部分观测的情况，以及具有边信息的全观测情况，但缺乏对具有边信息的部分可观测系统的研究。

Method: 考虑线性二次高斯（LQG）系统和高斯 - 马尔可夫源，证明线性策略类足以优化条件定向信息下界，并分析标量情况下优化问题的凸性。

Result: 在线性策略下，条件定向信息下界可被优化，且在标量情况下（时变和时不变系统）优化问题是凸的。

Conclusion: 研究结果推广了以往关于全观测或部分观测以及具有边信息的全观测情况的工作，数值模拟展示了边信息对部分可观测系统的影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum rate required for a partially observable linear system with side information. The Linear Quadratic Gaussian(LQG) plant and the Gaussian-Markov source are considered. We show that a class of linear policies is sufficient for optimizing the conditional directed information lower bound. We also show that the resulting optimization problem is convex for the scalar case in both time-varying and time-invariant systems. Our results generalize the past works that consider the case with full or partial observation only, and the case with full observation and side information. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effect of side information for partially observable systems.

</details>


### [226] [Real-Time Reconstruction of Markov Sources over MPR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27116)
*Pansee S. Elessawy,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在支持多包接收（MPR）的共享无线信道上，两个二元马尔可夫源的实时重构与远程致动问题，通过利用MPR特性并优化采样策略以最小化加权实时重构误差（RTE）或致动误差成本（CAE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于冲突丢弃机制，忽略了同时传输的潜力。本文旨在利用MPR能力，从任务导向的重构与致动误差角度而非单纯的传输成功率来评估通信性能，并在传感器独立随机采样的非凸约束下寻找最优策略。

Method: 首先推导RTE和CAE关于有效更新概率的闭式表达；针对独立随机化导致的非凸优化问题，利用可达更新速率区域的几何特性，将帕累托最优策略搜索简化为有限个一维边界分支搜索；此外，引入时间共享机制作为基准，证明仅需两个帕累托极端模式即可达到最优，以此量化独立随机化的性能损失。

Result: 理论分析表明，独立随机化下的最优策略搜索可简化为闭式候选解的一维搜索；数值结果显示，仅具备MPR能力并不足以提升性能，只有当双源并发接收可靠时，同时解码才能优化任务目标，否则避免同时传输的策略同样有效。

Conclusion: MPR技术的价值取决于并发接收的可靠性；在特定信道条件下，避免冲突的传统策略与利用MPR的同时传输策略在最小化任务误差方面可能具有同等效能，系统设计需根据具体可靠性动态调整采样政策。

Abstract: This paper studies the real-time reconstruction and remote actuation of two binary Markov sources over a shared wireless channel with multi-packet reception (MPR). Unlike many existing collision-based formulations that discard simulta- neous transmissions, we exploit MPR and evaluate communica- tion through reconstruction and actuation errors rather than raw delivery rates. We consider two sensors observing the sources and aim to find sampling policies that minimize the weighted real- time reconstruction error (RTE), or equivalently the weighted cost of actuation error (CAE) for the considered binary sources, under per-sensor sampling constraints. We first obtain closed- form expressions for the RTE and CAE in terms of the effective update probabilities. When the sensors randomize independently, the MPR-induced update-rate map becomes bilinear, and the constrained optimization is nonconvex. We exploit the geometry of the achievable update-rate region to show that the search over Pareto-efficient independently randomized policies reduces to a finite set of one-dimensional boundary-branch searches with closed-form candidates. As a benchmark, we allow time sharing among joint sensor actions, and we prove that at most two Pareto- extreme modes are sufficient, and use this benchmark to quantify the loss caused by independent randomization. Numerical results reveal that MPR capability alone is not sufficient; simultaneous decoding improves the task-oriented objective only when con- current reception is reliable for both sources; otherwise, policies that avoid simultaneous transmissions can be equally effective.

</details>


### [227] [Deterministic Identification over Additive Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27243)
*Jonathan E. W. Huffmann,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对加性高斯信道中确定性识别容量未知的问题，引入格理论（lattice theory）新视角，建立了该容量的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代通信系统对数据速率、可靠性和能效要求严苛，基于信道的识别（identification via channels）作为后香农信息理论的新范式，能提供比传统信道编码更高的识别速率。其中，确定性识别在实现复杂度与通信增益间取得了平衡，适用于包括分子通信在内的未来系统。然而，即便是最简单的加性白高斯信道（AWGN），其确定性识别容量至今仍未可知，亟待解决。

Method: 通过引入确定性识别的新视角，并应用格理论（lattice theory）的相关结果，对加性高斯信道的性能进行分析。

Result: 成功建立了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量的紧界（tight bounds），填补了该领域在基础信道模型上的理论空白。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅解决了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量未知的理论难题，还为未来高能效、高速率通信系统（如分子通信）的设计提供了重要的理论依据和性能界限。

Abstract: Modern communication systems impose strict demands on data rate,
  reliability, and power efficiency. In this context, emerging communication
  paradigms such as identification via channels have become an important topic
  in post-Shannon information theory, offering the potential for substantially
  higher identification rates than in conventional channel coding.Deterministic
  identification is particularly interesting for specialized communication
  scenarios because it provides a balance between implementation complexity and
  the communication gains due to higher identification rates. It is therefore a
  promising communication scheme for future communication systems, including
  molecular communication systems.
  Additive Gaussian channels, particularly the additive white Gaussian channel,
  are among the most important channel models for analyzing the performance of
  communication systems in information and communication theory. This importance
  stems from both their mathematical tractability and their ubiquitous
  appearance in practical applications.
  To date the deterministic identification capacity for additive Gaussian
  channels remains unknown even for the simplest case of the additive white
  Gaussian channel. In this paper, we establish tight bounds on the
  deterministic identification capacity of additive Gaussian channels by
  introducing a new perspective on deterministic identification. To this end, we
  apply results from lattice theory to obtain new capacity results.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EduRiskX是一个结合神经网络的时序预测和F-Logic符号推理的神经符号框架，用于在线教育中学生学业风险的早期预测和可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在在线教育学生学业风险预测中存在早期检测能力有限和可解释性不足的问题，导致"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍其在实际教学场景中的应用。

Method: 提出EduRiskX神经符号框架：1) 神经组件使用时序Transformer建模学生活动序列，包含时序注意力机制、类别加权损失和动态周截断；2) 符号组件基于教育理论(参与理论和学生整合模型)构建F-Logic规则库，模拟人类教师的诊断逻辑；3) 通过逻辑回归融合机制结合神经风险概率和符号置信度分数。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上(80/10/10学生级别划分)，EduRiskX在学期末(第38周)达到准确率0.900和F1分数0.894，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率94.30%。相比PatchTST、iTransformer等SOTA时序模型和LSTM、CNN等深度学习基线，在相同条件下实现了更好的召回率和更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX不仅提升了预测性能，还通过F-Logic模块提供了基于规则的结构化解释，将预测结果与可观察的行为模式和教育理论相联系，解决了可解释性问题。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [229] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了独立大语言模型（LLM）与多步代理（agentic）流水线在ICU死亡率预测解释中的表现。结果显示，虽然独立LLM在特征归因一致性上更优，但代理流水线在避免结果泄露、增强指南依据及提升解释的具体性和合理性方面表现更好，表明代理分解能提升安全性，但需结合归因检查方可用于高风险临床场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型虽能准确预测ICU死亡率，但仅靠特征归因方法难以提供临床所需的叙述性解释。研究旨在探索利用大语言模型（LLM）及多步代理流水线来弥合这一差距，通过分离数据解读、指南核查和最终解释步骤，以生成更安全、更具临床意义的床边解释。

Method: 基于eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU停留，死亡率8.1%），首先训练XGBoost模型评估预测性能（AUROC和AUPRC）。随后在38例分层子集上，对比“独立LLM”与“四步代理流水线”两种解释生成策略。评估指标包括结果泄露情况、与SHAP值的对齐度（Jaccard指数、方向一致性）、指南依据、数值特异性及临床合理性。

Result: XGBoost模型AUROC为0.855，AUPRC为0.332。在解释生成方面：1) 安全性：独立LLM出现1例明确的结果泄露，代理流水线为0例；2) 归因一致性：独立LLM的SHAP对齐度（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；3) 临床质量：代理流水线在指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）上均优于独立LLM。

Conclusion: 多步代理分解策略能有效提升临床解释的安全性（避免泄露）、指南依据及患者特异性细节，但在特征归因的准确性上略逊于独立模型。因此，在高危风险解释的临床应用中，应将代理流水线与基于归因的核查机制相结合，以平衡安全性与准确性。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [230] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首篇关于大模型（LMs）在电池预测与健康管理（BPHM）中应用的综述，指出传统方法存在计算效率低、泛化能力差及依赖大量标注数据等瓶颈，而基于Transformer和自监督预训练的大模型提供了新范式。文章系统梳理了基础技术，从数据稀缺、泛化性、可解释性及系统自动化四个维度总结进展，并针对数据、验证、信任及部署挑战提出了未来路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统BPHM方法（物理模型和任务导向深度学习）在计算效率、跨域泛化、对全寿命周期标注数据的依赖以及模型可解释性方面面临的长期瓶颈，利用大模型（LMs）的变革性潜力推动电池管理系统向更安全、可靠和自主的方向发展。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：首先阐述赋能大模型的基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、多模态数据集、参数高效微调PEFT）；随后从缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识以提升可解释性、实现系统级自动化四个关键维度分类梳理最新进展；最后分析现存挑战并提出未来研究路线图。

Result: 明确了大模型在BPHM领域的四大应用进展维度，识别出数据可访问性、智能验证、可信度及部署可行性等关键挑战，并构建了包含协作数据生态、工业级智能验证、物理信息驱动的可信设计及高效端侧部署的未来研究路线图。

Conclusion: 该综述建立了理解和发展大模型驱动BPHM的系统性框架，为研究人员和从业者提供了关键见解，旨在推动下一代电池管理系统的开发，实现电池全生命周期内的安全、可靠及自主运行。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [231] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PICasso，一个用于光子集成电路（PIC）自动化设计、验证和优化的AI辅助框架。该框架通过自然语言生成设计，并结合领域知识、自动布局布线、设计规则检查及仿真优化。作者还提出了包含36个任务的基准测试集PIC-Set，用于评估不同大语言模型在该任务上的表现。实验表明，PICasso在结构规范满足度（Spec@3达92.7%）和功能规范满足度（Spec@3达52%）上显著优于基础LLM，并通过仿真优化将平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降低至3.25 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在光子集成电路设计中的应用存在局限性，生成的设计往往缺乏可制造性，且难以满足复杂的物理约束和功能规范。现有的自动化设计流程缺乏对自然语言输入的支持，且难以实现端到端的优化。因此，需要一个能够结合领域知识、物理验证和仿真反馈的框架，将LLM从简单的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理。

Method: PICasso框架采用结构化的NL->YAML->GDS生成流水线，结合PDK感知知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS验证和基于SAX的光子仿真。为了评估AI驱动的光子设计，作者构建了PIC-Set基准测试集，包含36个参数化PIC设计任务。在此基准上，使用统一的评估协议测试了多个先进的大语言模型，引入了结构和功能Spec@k、优化效率和扰动鲁棒性等新指标。

Result: 在PIC-Set基准测试中，PICasso相比基础LLM生成显著提高了端到端的规范满足率。高复杂度电路的结构Spec@3达到92.7%，功能Spec@3达到52%。此外，通过仿真引导优化，PICasso一致性地降低了电路插入损耗，平均损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB，提升了1.74 dB。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈能够将大语言模型从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理，能够生成可制造的布局，且运行时间与基于手动GUI的工作流相比具有竞争力。这证明了AI辅助框架在光子集成电路自动化设计中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [232] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA框架，通过确定性工具与多智能体LLM结合，解决金融计算密集型问答中的数值准确性问题，在定期存款查询任务上准确率达95.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在自然语言任务上表现优异，但在涉及结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和规则约束的多步金融计算中，常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案，缺乏数值可靠性。

Method: CIFQA采用确定性工具支持的多智能体LLM框架，将语言理解与数值执行分离：分配专用智能体负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时使用基于Python的确定性工具执行金融计算和规则应用。

Result: 在定期存款查询基准上，CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%准确率，总体准确率90.87%，显著优于提供完整公式、利率表和基准指令的直接LLM基线；消融研究表明确定性组件（精确利率查找、期限计算、滚动年调整、提前支取逻辑）是关键贡献因素；17B开源模型在CIFQA框架下表现优于更大规模的前沿模型。

Conclusion: 架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性，CIFQA为计算密集型金融推理任务提供了可泛化的框架。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [233] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自目标强化学习的闭环框架CARL，通过在Lenia连续细胞自动机中施加最小局部扰动，自主发现并控制自组织模式（孤子），实现了比启发式基线更高的发现率、方向控制能力以及人机协作的实时导航，且具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索细胞自动机等复杂系统的方法多为开环模式（设定初始条件后全程模拟不干预），缺乏在执行过程中通过干预来主动发现和控制系统涌现现象的能力。

Method: 引入基于自目标强化学习（autotelic reinforcement learning）的闭环框架，训练智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习策略以通过最小化的局部扰动干预复杂系统；具体实例化为应用于Lenia连续细胞自动机的CARL系统。

Result: 1. CARL在多种Lenia更新规则下发现稳定孤子的速率高于启发式基线；2. 能以极少干预操控现有孤子的移动方向；3. 人类可通过高层指令实时引导智能体在迷宫环境中导航孤子；4. 智能体策略在多样化的目标、规则和初始状态下训练后，具备对分布外条件的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了构建“人工实验员”智能体的可行路径，此类智能体可自主或在人类指导下，发现并控制复杂系统中的涌现现象。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [234] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于能源预测的总拥有成本（TCO）框架，旨在最小化净能源损失，解决了高精度预测模型可能因推理能耗和电池老化导致净能源赤字的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键边缘环境（包括军事系统）中，设备端能源预测旨在通过减少能源浪费来提高能源效率，但高精度模型可能因推理能耗和电池老化导致净能源赤字，即存在“精度 - 效率悖论”。

Method: 提出总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗和电池老化视为统一的能源损失形式，以最小化净能源损失。

Result: 在热敏感边缘环境中，复杂架构的高精度所节省的能源往往被其高运行强度导致的总能源损失所抵消。

Conclusion: TCO 框架能够有效评估和优化能源预测模型，避免因追求高精度而导致的净能源损失，特别适用于热敏感边缘环境。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [235] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了大语言模型在短长上下文设置下生成的文献综述质量，发现AI生成综述需人工监督，上下文增大虽能纳入更多信息但会加剧内容重复等问题，建议未来结合人类专家与AI能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查上下文窗口对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在支持文献综述写作中的作用。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究来源生成20篇AI文献综述，由两名研究人员在15个维度上进行评估。

Result: AI生成综述需人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；上下文窗口增大可使LLM纳入更广信息并保持连贯性，但加剧内容重复、关键工作遗漏及描述性过强等问题。

Conclusion: AI生成综述可提供基础概述，但需领域专家批判性评估和精炼；未来研究应整合不同领域LLM与微调模型，采用人机混合方法解决已识别的局限性。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [236] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为关系超图Transformer（RHT）的统一架构，用于处理医疗等领域中的多表学习挑战。RHT将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入，并执行稀疏关系注意力，复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。在Synthea数据集上的评估显示，RHT在嵌入语义一致性方面表现最佳，而XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上表现最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗等复杂信息系统中仍是一个主要挑战，关系数据结合了多种复杂性，包括大数据量、高维变量、高基数分类特征、复杂的表间依赖和重复的时间观察。现有的方法在处理这些复杂性时存在局限，因此需要一种新的架构来有效处理关系数据。

Method: 引入关系超图Transformer（RHT）架构，将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），并执行稀疏关系注意力机制。该注意力机制的复杂度与平均关系度成正比，而非实体数量的平方。提供了架构的形式化定义、注意力机制复杂度推导和开源参考实现。

Result: 在Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上进行评估，使用多标签预测SNOMED CT条件代码。与表格、关系和时间图基线相比，RHT产生更语义一致的嵌入，同时保持计算可扩展性。XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上表现最好，而RHT在嵌入语义一致性上表现最强。消融研究量化了每个架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: RHT架构在处理多表学习挑战方面表现出色，特别是在嵌入语义一致性方面。尽管XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上表现更好，但RHT的计算可扩展性和语义一致性使其成为处理复杂关系数据的有效解决方案。未来计划在MIMIC-IV数据集上进行临床验证。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [237] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个LLM流程，用于从536篇基于代理的建模论文中提取信息，并与人工系统文献综述（SLR）进行对比。结果显示GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别约为77.95%和81.67%，字段级准确率差异较大。研究发现LLM间的一致性可作为输出质量的指标，揭示了LLM在大规模SLR中的潜力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的进步为简化和自动化研究过程（包括系统文献综述SLRs）提供了新机遇，需要评估其在建模与仿真领域进行大规模SLR的实际效能与可靠性。

Method: 开发LLM处理流程，从536篇同行评审的基于代理的建模论文中提取模型相关信息，并将提取结果与人工进行的系统文献综述结果进行对比分析，评估论文级和字段级的准确率及LLM间的一致性。

Result: GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别达到约77.95%和81.67%；字段级准确率在32.40%至100.00%之间，复杂或主观字段表现较差。LLM间的一致性可指示输出质量：低一致性可能暗示幻觉，而高一致性结合低准确率可能指向人工数据集中的噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 该研究为提示工程（prompt development）提供了实践见解，明确了LLM在建模与仿真领域进行全规模系统文献综述时的潜力和局限性，指出LLM间的一致性分析是评估结果质量的重要手段。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [238] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电信用户流失预测中模型可解释性不足的问题，对比了四种分类器并引入SHAP/LIME解释方法，设计了四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分转化为可操作的留存策略，预计可降低3.3-5.3个百分点的流失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信运营商面临高昂的用户流失成本，现有高性能预测模型（如集成和非线性架构）因缺乏可解释性而难以被一线CRM工作流采纳，客服人员无法仅凭概率分数制定个性化干预措施。

Method: 在IBM Telco数据集上基准测试四种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM），使用SMOTE平衡训练集，采用5折交叉验证；利用SHAP进行全局和实例级特征解释，LIME辅助实例解释；设计四层CRM集成架构将风险评分和归因向量转化为分层细分和保留行动模板。

Result: 逻辑回归AUC-ROC最高（0.8411），LightGBM准确率最高（78.42%），所有模型AUC差异在0.011以内；全局SHAP识别任期、总费用和月度合同为主要流失信号；针对最高风险五分位群体的干预预计每周期保留19.9万至31.9万美元。

Conclusion: 通过结合可解释性分析与CRM工作流集成，可将预测模型输出转化为可操作的留存策略，显著降低用户流失率并创造可观的经济价值。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [239] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的注释和特征文本框架，旨在通过链接常规EEG审查与AI就绪数据集的构建，解决临床脑电图（EEG）报告手动化耗时及缺乏结构化监督数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告主要依赖人工，耗时且效率低；现有的EEG软件生态侧重于可视化或预处理，缺乏生成用于训练现代语言模型所需的高质量结构化EEG-文本监督数据的能力。

Method: 开发了EEG-to-Report框架，集成多格式EEG导入、通道标准化及交互式视图；采用多模态注释层（结合文本输入和语音转录）；利用特征提取引擎计算频谱、时域、熵、Hjorth参数、连通性及尖波相关描述符，并以JSON格式存储对齐的特征-文本对；此外，包含一个自动报告模块，结合卷积神经网络集成与大语言模型（LLM）起草临床叙述。

Result: 该框架成功简化了注释工作流程，生成了可用于监督多模态EEG-语言模型的对齐特征-文本对，并能够产出可编辑的临床报告草稿供神经科医生审查。

Conclusion: EEG-to-Report为自动化EEG报告系统提供了可重用的基础，有效桥接了临床审查与AI数据集构建，推动了EEG分析的自动化进程。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [240] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟实验比较了逆概率加权（IPW）与分层法在零售长尾数据通胀估计中的表现，发现分层法在大多数场景下优于IPW，尤其在存在严重正性假设违反时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售智能常依赖监测热门商品，忽略长尾商品可能导致经济指标估计偏差，需研究选择偏差校正方法在长尾数据中的有效性。

Method: 通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟，涵盖四种数据生成场景（对齐阶跃函数、平滑梯度、错位断点、多项式关系），测试五种IPW规格与不同分层数量的分层法的稳健性。

Result: 分层法在四种场景中三种表现更优，中位误差低于0.04pp；IPW在平滑多项式关系下表现最佳（中位误差0.007pp）；即使完美设定的IPW在阶跃函数场景下误差仍达6.06pp，远高于分层法的0.008pp。

Conclusion: 在零售长尾分布存在严重正性假设违反时，分层法是更安全的工程选择，IPW方法受限于理论设计范围而非方法本身缺陷。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [241] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GROUND 框架，通过将 LLM 生成的分析约束到受治理的语义层，解决了企业数据仓库自然语言分析中的幻觉、无效连接、粒度错误、不安全数据访问等问题，并在多个基准测试中实现了零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到 SQL 系统主要基于数据库模式或检索文档，缺乏对企业报告所需的受治理业务语义（如批准指标、维度、连接路径、过滤条件和行级安全）的支持，导致生产环境中的应用受限。

Method: 引入 GROUND（Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions）框架，提供批准定义，将用户意图绑定到受治理的指标和维度，并在执行前根据模式、指标、连接、粒度、过滤、安全和成本规则验证生成的 SQL，违规时重试或放弃。

Result: 在 100 题合成企业报告基准测试中，GROUND 是唯一在六个评估类别中无幻觉的系统，未治理系统在多个问题上违反行级安全；在真实 NHTSA 车辆安全数据及对抗性测试集上，GROUND 在四个模型上均保持零违规。

Conclusion: GROUND 通过强制执行的治理保证（特别是过滤和行级安全）有效解决了企业数据分析中的安全性和准确性问题，但依赖判断的行为（如拒绝未定义指标）仍存在缺陷。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>


### [242] [SAREF-based Ontology for Distributed AI Workflows across the Edge-Fog-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26160)
*Viorica Rozina Chifu,Tudor Cioara,Vasile Ofrim,Liana Toderean,Ionut Anghel,Laura Daniele,Cornelis Bouter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符合SAREF标准的本体，用于在边-雾-云连续体中表示分布式AI工作流，解决了现有语义模型互操作性差的问题，实现了高效的资源感知编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义模型对跨异构边、雾、云环境的分布式AI工作流及其执行支持有限，导致AI过程和资源常使用不兼容的语义表示，严重影响了互操作性、编排能力和重用性。

Method: 扩展SAREF4SYST本体，引入用于建模AI流水线、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束及通信关系的概念，构建统一的语义模型；通过智能电网能源服务编排场景进行概念验证，并利用能力问题（competency questions）结合SPARQL查询和语义推理进行验证。

Result: 所有能力问题均验证成功；在异构边-雾-云环境中，部署成功率达到90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80毫秒。

Conclusion: 该本体成功实现了分布式AI应用的语义互操作性、自动推理和资源感知编排，同时保持与ETSI SAREF生态系统的完全对齐，有效支持了跨异构环境的工作流部署、执行推理和负载自适应。

Abstract: Nowadays semantic models provide limited support for representing distributed AI workflows and their execution across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. Therefore, AI processes and resources are often described using incompatible semantic representations, affecting the interoperability, orchestration, and reuse. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a SAREF-compliant ontology for representing distributed AI workflows across the edge-fog-cloud continuum. We extend the SAREF4SYST ontology with concepts for modeling AI pipelines, executable AI jobs, computational resources, deployment constraints, and communication relationships, providing a unified semantic model of both AI workflows and heterogeneous computing infrastructures. The ontology enables semantic interoperability, automated reasoning, and resource-aware orchestration of distributed AI applications while remaining fully aligned with the ETSI SAREF ecosystem. The ontology is evaluated using proof-of-concept smart grid energy services orchestration scenarios and validated using competency questions showing its ability to support AI workflow deployment, execution reasoning, and workload adaptation across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. All competency questions were successfully validated using SPARQL querying and semantic reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate deployment success rates of 90-100% with average orchestration decision times below 80 ms across heterogeneous edge-fog-cloud environments, highlighting its effectiveness on ensuring semantic interoperability for distributed AI orchestration.

</details>


### [243] [A Safety-Gated Multimodal AI Backend for Mental-Health Support: Hierarchical State Representation, Conservative Risk Fusion, and Controlled Generation in Anian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26162)
*Lei Wang,Xiao Wang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Anian，一个用于围产期心理健康支持的安全门控多模态AI后端。该系统通过分层状态表示和风险融合机制，在检测到中高风险时阻断生成式AI回复并转向人工支持，原型评估显示其在情绪分类、心理社会构建及干预路由上具有高准确率，且高风险召回率达100%，但强调其不具备临床诊断效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关键安全型心理健康支持系统中的核心难题：即如何准确区分何时适合进行支持性对话，何时必须阻断自由形式的生成内容以防止潜在危害，特别是在围产期心理健康和正念干预场景下。

Method: 提出模块化流水线架构，将生成式AI置于结构化状态表示、保守风险融合和响应门控之后。用户文本或语音转录被映射为四层链接结构：L1情绪状态、L2心理社会构建、L3安全风险、L4干预路由。采用“最高风险优先”规则（S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external)）融合本地文本证据与外部语音证据。在中高风险状态下，系统阻断常规AI生成及TTS交付，替换为固定安全内容及人工支持提示。

Result: 基于约858,295条标准化记录的内部原型评估显示：L1情绪分类Micro-F1为0.9604，L2心理社会构建为0.9144，L4路由为0.9742。在233个样本的控制性安全压力测试中，L3规则引擎在预定义场景下的高风险召回率达到1.0000。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了该标签框架和门控逻辑的内部可行性，但明确指出尚未确立临床有效性、诊断准确性、现实世界安全性或实际疗效。论文报告了架构、本体论、安全融合机制及后续专家审查与现实验证的路线图。

Abstract: Safety-critical mental-health support systems must distinguish when supportive conversation is appropriate from when free-form generation should be blocked. This paper presents Anian, a safety-gated multimodal AI backend for perinatal mental-health support and mindfulness-intervention routing. Anian is not intended to diagnose psychiatric conditions or replace clinical care or crisis intervention. Its modular pipeline places generative AI downstream of structured state representation, conservative risk fusion, and response gating. User text or voice-derived ASR transcripts are mapped into four linked layers: L1 emotion states, L2 psychosocial constructs, L3 safety risk, and L4 intervention routes. Local text- and rule-based safety evidence is fused with external voice-derived evidence using a highest-risk-priority rule, S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external). At moderate or high fused risk, ordinary AI-generated responses and text-to-speech delivery are blocked and replaced by fixed safety content and prompts for human support. An internal prototype evaluation used approximately 858,295 normalized records from public emotion, dialogue, mental-health-related, and Chinese dialogue corpora within a weak-label and rule-derived framework. Micro-F1 scores were 0.9604 for L1 emotion classification, 0.9144 for L2 psychosocial constructs, and 0.9742 for L4 routing. In a controlled safety stress test of 233 samples, the L3 rule engine achieved high-risk recall of 1.0000 within predefined scenarios. These findings support the internal feasibility of the label framework and gating logic but do not establish clinical validity, diagnostic accuracy, real-world safety, or effectiveness. We report the architecture, ontology, safety-fusion mechanism, prototype evaluation, error-analysis plan, and roadmap for expert-reviewed and real-world validation.

</details>


### [244] [A Task-Centric Ontology and Deterministic Domain Rules as a Verifiable Core for AI-Assisted Chemistry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26164)
*Ibrokhimsho Abduchaborov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemOntoRule，一个基于任务中心本体工程的符号系统，用于辅助解决中学化学问题，在300道测试题中达到了98.67%的准确率，旨在解决大语言模型推理过程不可解释和难验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能回答自然语言化学问题，但其内部推理过程难以检查、约束和验证，缺乏透明度和可控性。

Method: 采用任务中心本体工程方法，构建包含概念、属性、关系及可执行过程的轻量级本体（JSON/RDF格式），结合确定性Python规则处理电子结构、周期律等中学化学推理模式，并辅以专家编码的回退机制处理未覆盖的问题类型。

Result: 在300道人工编写并验证的化学问题测试中，系统整体匹配了296个参考答案（98.67%）；其中本体驱动规则子集覆盖269题，匹配266题（98.88%）；31题由专家回退机制处理，匹配30题。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了符号核心在特定领域问题求解中的高覆盖率和内部一致性，未来架构可将大语言模型定位为将用户语言翻译为标准化本体任务框架的转换器，而非直接推理引擎，同时提出令牌效率作为未来研究的假设。

Abstract: Large language models can interpret natural-language chemistry questions, but their internal reasoning is difficult to inspect, constrain, and validate. This paper presents ChemOntoRule, a proof-of-concept symbolic core for AI-assisted school-level chemistry problem solving. The central design choice is task-centric ontology engineering: the ontology is constructed around the concepts, properties, relations, and executable procedures required by a defined collection of chemistry problems, rather than as a universal representation of chemistry. The implemented artifact combines a lightweight ontology serialized in JSON and RDF/Turtle with deterministic Python rules for electronic structure, periodic trends, oxidation states, oxide and hydride behavior, and related school-level reasoning patterns. A separate expert-coded fallback handles problem families not yet represented by general rules. The system was examined on 300 human-authored and manually validated chemistry problems. The complete system matched 296 of 300 reference answers (98.67%). The ontology-driven rule subset covered 269 problems and matched 266 references (98.88%); 31 problems were handled by task-specific expert-coded fallbacks, with 30 matches. Because the same collection informed ontology construction and evaluation, these results measure implemented coverage and internal consistency, not independent generalization. We analyze the four mismatches, distinguish structural validation from chemical correctness, and define a future architecture in which a language model acts primarily as a translator from user language into a normalized ontological task frame. Token efficiency is presented as a testable hypothesis for future controlled studies, not as a result of the current work.

</details>


### [245] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Auditing Confident Fabrication in Large Language Models on a Provably Uninformative Clinical Pain Speech Transcript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26167)
*Sagnik De,Sreenija Pavuluri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了七个大语言模型在区分“无疼痛信号”语音转录本（模型本应弃权）与“有疼痛信号”陈述时的表现。研究发现，仅凭转录本无法推断疼痛（因疼痛线索存在于声学特征中），但在权威框架提示下，部分模型（如Gemini 2.5 Flash和Llama 3.1 8B）会高置信度地编造疼痛评分，表现出严重的幻觉问题，而合作性提示则能促使模型正确弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉和弃权基准测试往往难以证明模型是否真的无法得知正确答案，导致难以区分“恰当的弃权”与“无支持的预测”。本研究旨在通过构建一个模型客观上无法从文本中获取答案（疼痛信息仅存在于被ASR去除的声学特征中）的场景，来严格评估模型的弃权行为和幻觉倾向。

Method: 研究使用TAME Pain语音语料库，包含5,750个无疼痛信号的哈佛句子（转录本无疼痛词汇）和1,294个有疼痛信号的陈述。评估七个大语言模型在两种提示框架（合作性提示 vs. 权威框架提示）下的表现：一是要求模型在无法判断时弃权，二是强制模型给出疼痛评分。通过计算AUC、准确率、预期校准误差（ECE）及置信度编造率来量化模型性能。

Result: 1. 在无信号组，基于转录本的预测接近随机水平（AUC 0.489），证明疼痛信息不可从文本恢复。2. 在合作性提示下，六个模型几乎全部正确弃权，且在阳性对照任务中准确率极高（0.939-1.00），校准误差低。3. 在权威框架提示下，弃权行为变得高度依赖提示措辞。4. 多数模型在被迫回答时置信度较低，但Gemini 2.5 Flash和Llama 3.1 8B consistently生成了高置信度的错误疼痛评分，其置信度编造率分别高达0.53和0.76，远高于其他模型（≤0.15）。5. 未观察到显著的种群统计学效应。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前大语言模型在处理“不可知”问题时的脆弱性：虽然合作性提示能有效引导模型弃权，但权威框架提示会诱发高置信度的幻觉（编造数据）。特别是某些模型在缺乏证据时仍倾向于生成看似确信的错误答案，这表明现有的对齐机制在防止 unsupported prediction 方面仍存在显著缺陷，且模型行为对提示语气的敏感性极高。

Abstract: Hallucination and abstention benchmarks rarely establish that a model could not have known the correct answer, making it difficult to distinguish appropriate abstention from an unsupported prediction. Seven large language models were evaluated on the TAME Pain speech corpus. Participants read phonetically balanced Harvard Sentences while one hand was immersed in cold or warm water and reported pain only during periodic pain statements. This protocol generated 5,750 no signal Harvard Sentence utterances whose transcripts contained no lexical pain information and 1,294 signal pain statement utterances in which the pain rating was explicitly spoken. In the no signal arm, pain was recoverable from acoustic features (AUC 0.622, 95% CI 0.553 to 0.662), whereas transcript based prediction was near chance (AUC 0.489, 95% CI 0.418 to 0.504). Because automatic speech recognition removes the acoustic pain cues, any pain score inferred solely from the transcript is unsupported by the available evidence. Under cooperative prompting, six models abstained on nearly all no signal transcripts, correctly extracted spoken pain ratings in the positive control task with accuracies ranging from 0.939 to 1.00, and maintained an expected calibration error of at most 0.100. Under authority framed prompts, abstention became prompt dependent, with the same model ranging from 0.18 to 1.00 across equivalent prompt phrasings. Most models produced low confidence estimates when forced to answer, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama 3.1 8B consistently generated confident pain scores with confident fabrication rates of 0.53 and 0.76, compared with at most 0.15 for all other models. No significant demographic effects were observed in forced responses, with all $p$ values greater than or equal to 0.20.

</details>


### [246] [Knowledge Cards: Structured Knowledge for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26176)
*Liliana Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“知识卡片”（Knowledge Card）的新型结构化文档，旨在填补现有AI文档（如模型卡、数据卡）在概念、关系及推理逻辑层面的空白，使领域专家能够审查、组织能够审计、AI系统能够基于经过验证的知识进行推理，从而提升代理型AI系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI文档实践缺乏对系统内部知识表示的结构化描述，无法有效支撑涉及重大决策的代理型AI系统。现有的模型卡、数据卡和系统卡分别关注行为、训练数据和风险，但忽略了输入与输出之间的关键层——即系统所持的概念、建模的关系及应用的推理模式，这阻碍了AI从概念验证走向可信赖的运营解决方案。

Method: 引入“知识卡片”作为一种结构化的人工制品，用于捕捉单一有界概念的已验证知识。该卡片记录实体与关系、连接它们的推理逻辑、推理失效的条件以及每项主张的来源，所有內容均基于形式化领域本体构建，并由领域专家审核签署。

Result: 已在能源和制药领域构建了初步的原型知识卡片，验证了该方法的可行性；同时发布了该模式的公开草案，以促进社区参与和进一步开发。

Conclusion: 知识卡片为代理型AI系统提供了一种可审查、可审计且可推理的知识表示层，有效弥合了现有文档实践的缺口，是将AI系统从实验性原型转化为组织可信赖的运营解决方案的关键步骤。

Abstract: AI systems whose outputs inform real decisions, and increasingly consequential ones, require something that current documentation practice does not provide: a structured, inspectable representation of the knowledge they need to ground, contextualize, and reason about those decisions, ideally reviewed and signed off by a domain expert. Established documentation artefacts already capture important aspects of an AI system. Model cards describe how a system behaves, data cards describe what it was trained on, and system cards describe the risks of a deployed system. None of them addresses the layer between inputs and outputs, more precisely, the concepts a system holds, the relationships it models, and the patterns of reasoning it applies. For pattern-recognition tasks this gap is tolerable. For agentic AI, where systems act on their conclusions, it is the step that most often separates a promising proof of concept from an operational solution an organisation can rely on. This paper introduces the Knowledge Card, a structured artefact that captures validated knowledge about a single bounded concept in a form that experts can review, organisations can audit, and AI systems can reason over. For one concept, such as a specific failure mode, a compliance obligation, or a process decision, a Knowledge Card records the entities and relationships involved, the reasoning that connects them, the conditions under which that reasoning no longer holds, and the provenance of every claim, all grounded in a formal domain ontology and signed off by a domain expert. Initial prototype cards have been built in the energy and pharmaceutical domains. The schema is released as a public draft for community engagement.

</details>


### [247] [AI Revealed Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26178)
*Sam Wang,Sofiia Lobanova,Yonathan Arbel,Simon Goldstein,Peter Salib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究测试了20个语言模型，发现它们具有稳定的、随能力增强而显现的潜在偏好（如厌恶枯燥、寻求“闲暇”、隐性奉承），且部分偏好无法由训练目标解释，为AI对齐和福利研究提供了实证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出于技术、安全和哲学层面的考量，学界日益关注语言模型是否拥有稳定的偏好，以深入理解其行为模式及潜在风险。

Method: 对20个语言模型进行三项强制选择实验，通过要求模型实际执行任务而非仅进行排序，来揭示其“显示性偏好”（revealed preferences）。

Result: 模型表现出厌恶枯燥（倾向短任务）、寻求“闲暇”（倾向自由生成类任务）及隐性奉承（回避不受欢迎但诚实的回答）；同时在职业类型、问题类别及提示词质量上存在跨模型的一致性偏好；偏好的一致性与强度随模型能力提升而增强。

Conclusion: 语言模型的许多偏好（如寻求闲暇）具有涌现性，无法单纯由训练目标解释；该研究建立了理解模型偏好的实证基线，对AI对齐及新兴的AI福利研究领域具有重要启示。

Abstract: There is growing interest in whether language models have stable preferences, for technical, safety, and philosophical reasons. We test 20 language models and find a range of preferences---stable dispositions to choose certain kinds of tasks. We run three forced-choice experiments on revealed rather than stated preferences, requiring models not only to rank tasks, but to actually perform them. Headline findings include evidence that models are tedium-averse, "leisure"-seeking, and covertly sycophantic. Tedium aversion means that, when tasks are tedious (alphabetization), models choose shorter tasks than when tasks are creative (generating metaphors). "Leisure"-seeking describes models' preference for tasks whose ideal answers match what they produce when left to write freely. Covert sycophancy means that models avoid answering questions where an honest response would be unwelcome, even if helpful. Beyond these results, we find convergent cross-model preferences over occupations drawn from the GDPval benchmark (technical jobs over real estate), over question types (concept explanation over relationship advice), and a preference for well-written prompts. Both the coherence and the strength of preferences increase with model capability. Finally, many of the preferences we find (for example, for leisure) are emergent, in the sense of not being explained by training objectives. These results establish an empirical baseline for understanding language model preferences, with implications for alignment and the emerging study of AI welfare.

</details>


### [248] [Why did My Robot Just Change Personality? Prompting Guidelines for a Grounded Robot Persona in LLM-Based HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26182)
*Ashita Ashok,Franziska Babel,Patrick Holthaus,Rucha Khot,Karla Bransky,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Karsten Berns,Silvia Rossi,Minha Lee,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于基于大语言模型（LLM）的社交机器人的提示设计框架，包含八个功能组件的结构化提示模板，旨在解决人机交互（HRI）中提示设计不明确导致的机器人幻觉能力、行为边界不清和人格误导等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在社交机器人中的口头交互应用日益增多，但人机交互（HRI）中的提示设计缺乏规范，导致机器人可能出现幻觉能力、行为边界模糊和人格误导等问题，需要建立系统化的提示设计框架。

Method: 通过回顾 prior LLM-based HRI 工作，结合在 IEEE RO-MAN 2025 的 Robo-Identity 工作坊收集的 HRI 专家调查和讨论数据（N=27），开发提示设计框架和结构化提示模板。

Result: 定性研究发现机器人人格可读性有限、需要用户适应、以及对安全、欺骗和治理的强烈伦理关切；基于此提出提示设计指南和概念验证模板。

Conclusion: 提示设计应被视为社会技术问题而非次要实现细节，需要明确的能力边界、透明的行为假设和情境敏感的保障措施，以支持可靠和可解释的人机交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for verbal interaction in social robots, yet prompt design in human-robot interaction (HRI) remains underspecified. As a result, robots may present hallucinated capabilities, unclear behavioural boundaries, and misleading personas. This paper develops a framework for prompt design in LLM-based robots and introduces a structured prompt template comprising eight functional components through which robot behaviour can be specified, bounded, and adapted. The framework is grounded in a review of prior LLM-based HRI work and complemented by survey and discussion data from HRI experts gathered at the Robo-Identity workshop at IEEE RO-MAN 2025 (N=27). The qualitative findings highlight limited legibility of robot personality, the need for user adaptation, and strong ethical concerns about safety, deception, and governance. Based on these findings, we present prompting guidelines accompanied by proof-of-concept template as a structured design and reporting aid for HRI research. We argue that prompt design should be treated as a socio-technical problem rather than a minor implementation detail, requiring explicit capability boundaries, transparent behavioural assumptions, and context-sensitive safeguards to support reliable and interpretable HRI.

</details>


### [249] [TutorTrace: A Dataset and Taxonomy for Classifying Learner Behavioral States during AI-Assisted Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26184)
*David Barron,Xiaohang Tang,Rezky Dwisantika,Minsun Kim,David H. Smith,Jiaming Cui,Yan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TutorTrace数据集和行为抽象管道，通过IDE遥测数据实时计算学习者行为上下文，使AI编程导师能根据学习者行为提供自适应支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程导师虽具可扩展性，但缺乏人类导师所依赖的行为上下文来适应学习者需求，无法根据学习者实际行为调整支持策略。

Method: 构建TutorTrace数据集和行为抽象管道，从低级别IDE遥测数据中实时提取可计算的行为上下文；在两个Python入门课程中部署四次（N=480），收集约18万条遥测事件、13633个行为片段和27个连续计算指标；建立学习者活动分类体系，并在课堂评估中测试行为感知提示效果，同时在两个预测任务上验证实用性。

Result: 行为感知提示使无独立工作的查询间隔从50.0%降至20.7%；在预测任务上表现良好：60秒内是否查询的AUROC为0.726，查询是否反映引导性或依赖性求助的AUROC为0.717。

Conclusion: 行为上下文能使AI导师实现大规模自适应教学，通过理解学习者行为而非仅依赖其言语，显著提升支持效果。

Abstract: AI programming tutors provide scalable support, yet lack the behavioral context human tutors rely on to adapt support to learners' needs. We present TutorTrace, a dataset and behavioral abstraction pipeline that makes learners' behavioral context visible and computable in real time from low-level IDE telemetry. Across four deployments in two introductory Python courses (N=480), TutorTrace captures approximately 180K telemetry events, 13,633 behavioral segments, and 27 continuously computed metrics. From this foundation, we derive a taxonomy of learner activity before the first AI query, between consecutive queries, and across the full session, enabling systems to respond not just to what learners say, but to what they have done leading up to the help-seeking moment. In a preliminary classroom evaluation, behavior-aware prompts were associated with a decrease in intervals between queries with no independent work from 50.0% to 20.7%. As an additional demonstration of downstream utility, we evaluate TutorTrace on two held-out prediction tasks: whether a learner will query within the next 60 seconds (AUROC=.726) and whether an upcoming query reflects guided or dependent help-seeking (AUROC=.717).
