Method: 首先对代表性MLLMs进行系统性量化方案分析，通过消融实验定位激活量化为主要瓶颈；进而提出残差回退量化（RFQ），在不修改架构的前提下，利用辅助量化残差路径重构激活值，显式建模并补偿量化误差。

Result: 实验表明MXFP8接近无损，而4比特量化性能下降严重；RFQ在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL模型上，于视频生成及推理任务中均能恢复MXFP4和HiF4量化损失的大部分性能，显著缩小与BF16基线的差距。

Conclusion: 激活量化是超低比特MLLMs的主要瓶颈，基于残差的激活重构策略是实现鲁棒4比特部署的有效且实用方案。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [356] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Zero框架，通过挑战者-求解者-评判者协同进化，在可验证和不可验证领域均实现自我进化，性能优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化语言模型是通往超级智能的有前景路径，可减少人类监督成本，但不可验证领域的自进化研究不足。

Method: 提出J-Zero统一框架，挑战者与求解者通过对抗交互协同进化，评判者利用预知顺序的偏好对进行协同适应，而非依赖自身评分。

Result: 在可验证领域平均提升4.2分，不可验证领域提升8.0分，至少十次迭代持续改进，而基线在两次迭代后性能下降。

Conclusion: J-Zero框架有效支持跨领域自改进，解决了不可验证领域自进化探索不足的问题，展现出持续迭代优势。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [357] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SimCast-S2S，一种基于潜在扩散模型的生成式框架，用于解决次季节至季节（S2S）降水预报中不确定性量化难、计算成本高及训练数据稀缺的三大瓶颈，其性能优于深度学习基线并可与顶尖业务系统媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S降水预报具有重要的经济社会影响，但受限于预测信号弱、不确定性高以及业务系统计算成本高昂导致的模拟保真度不足，现有数据驱动方法难以有效生成概率性集合预报。

Method: 提出SimCast-S2S框架：1) 采用扩散模型生成管道以从条件分布中有效采样，实现概率预报；2) 在变分自编码器（VAE）学习的紧凑潜在空间中操作，降低生成大规模集合的计算成本；3) 利用低秩适应（LoRA）进行迁移学习，先在大规模气候模拟数据上预训练，再在有限的再分析数据上微调。

Result: 在再分析数据上，SimCast-S2S的表现优于卷积神经网络和U-Net等深度学习基线；尽管仅使用部分大气输入变量且无后处理或偏差校正，其表现仍具有竞争力，甚至在许多情况下优于ECMWF-S2S等最先进业务系统。

Conclusion: 结合潜在生成建模与“模拟到再分析”的迁移学习，为数据驱动的概率性S2S降水预报提供了一条高效且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [358] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了多种机器学习与深度学习模型在可再生能源场景下的表现，发现随机树集成（如Extra Trees）在处理结构化波浪能转换器（WEC）数据时优势明显，而混合集成模型（RF BiLSTM）在结合非线性表格关系与时序动态时取得了最佳预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在全面评估传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习在不同可再生能源数据集（大规模WEC、16 WEC及风电场SCADA数据）下的性能，以确定不同架构在特定能源场景下的适用性。

Method: 选取三个数据集进行基准测试：大规模WEC数据集、16 WEC数据集及Penmanshiel风电场的10分钟SCADA运行数据。对比了包括随机森林、Extra Trees、MLP、STGCN及RF BiLSTM混合模型在内的多种算法，重点分析其在处理结构化布局数据、时空交互及时序动态方面的能力。

Result: 1. 针对结构化WEC布局数据，Extra Trees表现最强，相比MLP基线MAE降低约63.7%。2. STGCN通过显式学习时空交互，将MAE降至167.0 kW，R值达0.93。3. RF BiLSTM混合模型取得整体最佳预测精度，MAE为150.5 kW，相比独立LSTM降低约75%，优于STGCN约10%。

Conclusion: 不存在 universally 最优的单一AI架构：随机和提升集成模型适用于结构化WEC代理建模；当显式空间交互占主导时，图网络更具优势；而当非线性表格关系与时序动态共存时，集成循环混合模型提供了最佳平衡。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [359] [Robust Neural Stimulation Response Modeling Through Meta-Learning and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26649)
*Matthew J Bryan,Daniel C Muir,Felix Schwock,Azadeh Yazdan-Shahmorad,Rajesh P N Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将元学习和预训练应用于神经刺激响应建模，通过跨会话预训练显著降低了预测失败率并减少了校准需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的闭环神经刺激在帕金森病和感觉恢复等治疗应用中具有前景，但受限于两个障碍：1) 预测模型在部分会话中会出现灾难性预测失败；2) 每次会话的校准要求与临床约束不兼容。

Method: 扩展时间基函数模型(TBFMs)，采用基于模型无关元学习(MAML)的新架构和算法进行跨会话预训练，在两只非人类灵长类动物的初级感觉运动皮层的40次光遗传刺激会话上进行评估。

Result: 元学习显著减少灾难性预测失败：在1k校准集大小下，测试R平方<0.05的会话从16次(单会话训练)降至1次(MAML预训练)，预测区间显著变窄(p<0.05)；在匹配精度下校准需求减少50-90%。

Conclusion: 跨会话刺激响应结构足够一致以支持预训练，首次提供实证证据表明元学习方法对神经刺激可行，其鲁棒性和样本效率提升直接解决了部署基于模型刺激控制器的已知障碍。

Abstract: Objective: Model-based closed-loop neural stimulation holds promise for therapeutic applications ranging from Parkinson's disease to sensory restoration, but deployment has been limited by two obstacles: 1) forecasting models for predicting the consequences of stimulation fail catastrophically on a meaningful fraction of sessions, and 2) per-session calibration requirements are often incompatible with clinical constraints. We address both by demonstrating, for the first time, that meta-learning and pretraining can be applied to neural stimulation response modeling. Methods: Temporal basis function models (TBFMs) forecast state-dependent neural responses to stimulation. We extend TBFMs with cross-session pretraining using a novel architecture and algorithm based on model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML), evaluating them on 40 sessions of optogenetic stimulation in primary sensorimotor cortex of two non-human primates. Results: Meta-learning substantially reduces catastrophic forecast failure: for a 1k calibration set size, sessions with test R-squared < 0.05 drop from 16 of 40 (single-session training) to 1 (MAML-pretrained), and prediction intervals become significantly narrower (p < 0.05). Calibration requirements are reduced by 50-90% at matched accuracy, enabling experiments otherwise infeasible within clinical session-time constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate that cross-session structure in stimulation responses is consistent enough to support pretraining, providing the first empirical evidence that meta-learning approaches are viable for neural stimulation. Significance: The robustness and sample efficiency gains directly address known obstacles to deploying model-based stimulation controllers. Our results motivate community efforts to assemble standardized multi-site stimulation datasets and to further explore meta-learning for robust closed-loop stimulation.

</details>


### [360] [When Privacy Hurts Mergeability: Geometry-Aware Model Merging under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26655)
*Jin Liu,Junkang Liu,Ning Xi,Yinbin Miao,Dawei Wei,Ke Cheng,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了差分隐私模型合并的几何障碍，提出了DP-Merging框架，通过减少局部锐度和参考漂移来提升隐私模型的合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可以在不访问原始任务数据的情况下构建多任务模型，但发布的任务模型可能泄露隐私数据。差分隐私可以限制这种泄露，但其对模型合并的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 提出DP-Merging框架，使用DP兼容的锐度感知目标引导模型向更平坦的损失区域移动，并使用基于参考的对齐正则化器使任务模型靠近共享预训练初始化。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务上的实验表明，DP-Merging在不同隐私预算下 consistently 提升了私有合并模型的性能，同时保持了底层DP微调过程的隐私保证。

Conclusion: 通过减少局部曲率和参考漂移可以收紧合并导致的损失增加的上界，DP-Merging有效解决了差分隐私模型合并的几何障碍。

Abstract: Model merging promises to construct a single multi-task model from independently fine-tuned task models without accessing the original task data. This makes it attractive when task data cannot be centralized, but released task models may still leak private fine-tuning data. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled mechanism for limiting such leakage, yet its effect on model merging remains poorly understood. In this paper, we study the geometry of differentially private model merging and identify two geometric obstacles that make private task models difficult to merge: \emph{local sharpness}, which makes task losses sensitive to the parameter displacement induced by merging, and \emph{reference drift}, which measures the displacement of private task models from the shared pretrained initialization and amplifies cross-task interference. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{DP-Merging}, a geometry-aware framework that improves the mergeability of differentially private task models. DP-Merging uses a DP-compatible sharpness-aware objective to guide each private task model toward flatter loss regions, and a reference-based alignment regularizer to keep task models close to the shared pretrained initialization. We derive a merge-gap upper bound showing that reducing local curvature and reference drift tightens the bound on the loss increase induced by merging. Experiments on vision and language tasks across multiple privacy budgets show that DP-Merging consistently improves private merged-model performance while preserving the privacy guarantees of the underlying DP fine-tuning procedures.

</details>


### [361] [Simple Actors and Deep Critics for Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26659)
*Guhyeon Kang,Jaehwi Lee,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC方法，通过将计算容量从生成式Actor转移至深度Critic，在保持离线强化学习性能的同时，将推理延迟降低至扩散策略的1/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散或流匹配的离线RL Actor虽然能捕捉多模态行为，但推理时需多次去噪或积分，导致部署开销巨大；而Critic仅在训练时使用，理论上更适合承载深度网络以提升容量，但加深Critic会引发优化、自举噪声放大和值范围漂移等不稳定问题。

Method: 提出LAC（Light Actor, deep Critic）架构：使用轻量级确定性Actor，并针对深度Critic的三种失效模式引入对应改进：残差MLP主干解决优化问题，n步自举目标抑制噪声放大，分类交叉熵损失防止值范围漂移。

Result: 在OGBench基准上，LAC性能匹配最强的扩散和流匹配基线，同时实现高达4倍的推理延迟降低，效果媲美无需蒸馏的一步蒸馏策略，且该Critic设计可迁移至不同Actor参数化形式。

Conclusion: 将容量投资于深度Critic而非复杂Actor是提升离线RL推理效率的有效路径，通过特定的稳定性改进措施，LAC成功实现了高性能与低延迟的统一。

Abstract: Recent progress in offline reinforcement learning (RL) has been driven by expressive generative actors such as diffusion and flow-matching policies, which capture multimodal behavior in offline datasets. However, these actors require multiple denoising or integration steps per action and thus incur substantial overhead at every decision in deployment. In this work, we revisit where capacity should be invested in an offline actor--critic method. Since the critic is used only during training and is discarded at deployment while the actor runs at every decision step, allocating capacity to the critic rather than the actor is more favorable for inference-time efficiency. However, scaling MLP critics in offline RL is known to introduce several distinct instabilities that have, in practice, kept critics shallow. We identify three distinct failure modes that arise when critics are deepened in offline RL---optimization, bootstrap-noise amplification, and value-range drift---and address each with a corresponding ingredient: a residual MLP backbone, n-step bootstrap targets, and a categorical cross-entropy loss. Combining these ingredients with a lightweight deterministic actor, we propose LAC (Light Actor, deep Critic). On OGBench, LAC matches the strongest diffusion- and flow-matching baselines while achieving up to 4x lower inference latency, comparable to one-step distilled policies without distillation. Its critic recipe also transfers across actor parametrizations.

</details>


### [362] [Neural Regression with Embeddings for Numerical Attribute Prediction in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26729)
*Rupesh Sapkota,Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou,Moshood Yekini,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LitEm神经回归模型，使归纳知识图谱嵌入模型能预测数值属性，并通过联合训练框架提升链接预测性能及属性编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳知识图谱嵌入模型虽广泛应用于链接预测等任务，但大多忽略了知识图谱中丰富的数值属性，限制了其对现实世界多样化信息的表征能力。

Method: 提出LitEm神经回归模型以预测知识图谱内的数值属性，并设计联合训练框架，将先进的归纳嵌入模型与LitEm结合进行协同训练。

Result: 在FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K和Mutagenesis数据集上，LitEm在多数属性预测任务中取得最优或次优结果；联合训练显著提升了双线性模型的链接预测性能，并证实模型能以“字面感知”方式有效编码属性信息。

Conclusion: LitEm及其联合训练框架不仅实现了数值属性的精准预测，还通过引入字面感知机制增强了嵌入模型对属性信息的利用能力，证明了性能提升并非仅源于参数量增加。

Abstract: In recent years, transductive knowledge graph embedding models have been applied to tasks such as link prediction and query answering. Although knowledge graphs often contain rich numerical attributes, most embedding models neglect them, limiting their ability to represent real-world knowledge graphs with diverse information. In this work, we propose a neural regression model (LitEm) that enables transductive knowledge graph embedding models to predict numerical attributes within knowledge graphs. Experimental results demonstrate that LitEm achieves the best or second-best results on most attributes across FB15K-237, YAGO15K, DB15K, and Mutagenesis. Furthermore, we propose a co-training framework that jointly trains state-of-the-art transductive knowledge graph embedding models with LitEm, which improves link prediction performance mainly for bilinear models and simultaneously enables them to predict numerical attributes. In addition, the literal-awareness evaluation demonstrates that co-training helps models to encode and exploit attribute information in a "literal-aware'' manner, suggesting that the observed gains are not merely due to additional parameters. We publicly release our implementation at https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings.

</details>


### [363] [Rethinking Message Passing as Retrieval for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26732)
*Jintang Li,Yuhong Chen,Ruofan Wu,Binli Luo,Jiayi Ji,Hui Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RTA框架，用标签感知检索与传播替代结构消息传递，在多个文本属性图基准上性能匹配或超越GNN和图LLM基线，同时提升效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在经验上成功，但为何邻域聚合优于节点级MLP尚不明确，且该范式计算成本高、对图结构缺陷敏感。

Method: 提出检索增强视角的GNN：每层通过MLP结合节点表示与检索图上下文的排列不变摘要进行预测；设计RTA框架，以标签感知检索与传播替代结构消息传递。

Result: 理论证明检索聚合与softmax注意力消息传递关联，且检索上下文监督对错误检索异常值具有鲁棒性；实验显示RTA在多个基准上性能匹敌或超越强基线，效率与鲁棒性更优。

Conclusion: RTA通过检索增强机制有效替代传统消息传递，在保持性能的同时提升计算效率与结构鲁棒性，为GNN设计提供新视角。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically conceptualized as message-passing neural networks, yet it remains unclear why neighborhood aggregation reliably outperforms node-wise multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Despite its empirical success, this paradigm can be computationally expensive and sensitive to imperfect graph structures. In this work, we present a retrieval-augmented view of GNNs: each layer makes predictions by applying an MLP to a node representation together with a permutation-invariant summary of retrieved graph context. Motivated by this perspective, we propose RTA, a simple MLP-based framework that replaces structural message passing with label-aware retrieval and propagation. We provide theoretical insights that (i) connect retrieval-based aggregation to softmax-attention message passing, and (ii) establish the robustness of retrieved-context supervision to mis-retrieved outliers. Experiments on multiple text-attributed graph benchmarks show that RTA matches or even outperforms strong GNN and graph LLM baselines while improving efficiency and robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [364] [Self-Augmented Diffusion Guidance for Physics-Informed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26748)
*Akira Osaka,Naoya Takeishi,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散引导和自生成数据增强的物理信息扩散模型，通过解耦控制方程评估与训练采样过程，在无需每步求解方程的情况下，显著减少了生成样本与真实物理动力学之间的偏差，同时提升了生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型在生成物理现象的时空信号（如流体动力学时间序列图像）时，缺乏对底层物理定律约束的整合，导致生成样本虽视觉上合理，但在动力学上可能严重偏离真实情况。

Method: 提出一种物理信息扩散引导方法，利用自生成数据增强学习条件分布，该分布以偏离正确物理动力学的程度为条件；采样时显式设定偏离条件为零。该方法将控制方程的评估与扩散模型的训练及采样过程解耦，避免了去噪过程中每步求解方程的计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比标准扩散模型显著降低了生成结果与物理定律的偏差，并且能与现有物理约束扩散方法结合，进一步减少偏差，同时适用于计算昂贵的数值模拟问题并实现更快的样本生成。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过简单有效的物理信息引导机制，在保持生成效率的同时显著提升了物理一致性，为复杂物理系统的快速、高保真模拟提供了新途径。

Abstract: Diffusion models can be used to generate spatiotemporal signals of physical phenomena, such as time-series images of fluid dynamics. However, a major limitation of standard diffusion models is that they do not incorporate constraints derived from the underlying physical laws. Consequently, generated samples may appear visually plausible while deviating substantially from the true dynamics. In this study, we propose a simple yet effective physics-informed approach based on diffusion guidance with self-generated data augmentation. The proposed method learns the data distribution conditioned on the degree of deviation from the physically correct dynamics and generates samples by explicitly setting the deviation condition to be zero. The method decouples the evaluation of the governing equations from the diffusion model training and sampling processes, avoiding the need to solve the governing equations at every iteration of the denoising process. This design makes the method applicable to problems requiring computationally expensive numerical simulations and enables faster sample generation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly reduces the deviations compared with standard diffusion models but also achieves further reductions when combined with existing physics-constrained diffusion methods.

</details>


### [365] [Safety by Design: Realized-Cost Constraints for Contextual Bandits with Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26755)
*Spyros Dragazis,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“高概率约束UCB”的新算法，用于解决异方差环境下的上下文赌博机问题，该算法通过阶段性强概率约束来确保安全性，相比传统的期望成本约束方法，能显著减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多关键应用（如剂量选择、自动驾驶）中，安全性至关重要。现有的安全约束赌博机模型通常要求每个动作的期望成本低于阈值，但在异方差环境中（即动作不仅影响期望值，还影响结果的波动性），仅约束期望成本可能不足以保证安全，因为单次实现的成本仍可能 catastrophically 超出安全范围。

Method: 作者研究了具有单维连续动作的上下文赌博机，并引入了针对实现成本的阶段性强概率约束。提出了一种名为“高概率约束UCB”（High-Probability Constrained UCB）的乐观 - 悲观算法：在探索奖励时保持乐观，而在估计安全动作集时保持保守。针对线性和一般函数类模型分别进行了理论分析。

Result: 对于线性奖励和成本模型，证明了该算法具有紧致的 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$ 遗憾界；并将分析扩展到了基于eluder维度的一般函数类。实验结果表明，与基于期望成本约束的基线方法相比，强制执行实现成本的安全约束能大幅减少安全违规次数。

Conclusion: 在异方差设置下，通过强概率约束来控制实现成本而非仅仅期望成本，是确保上下文赌博机安全性的更有效方法；所提出的算法在理论上具有最优的遗憾界，并在实践中显著提升了安全性。

Abstract: Contextual bandits are a standard framework for sequential decision-making under uncertainty, with applications in clinical trials, dosage selection, recommendation systems, and autonomous systems. Safety is central in many of these applications, since a single unsafe decision in settings such as dosage selection or autonomous driving can have catastrophic consequences. A common way to model safety in bandit problems is to associate each action with both a reward signal and a cost signal, and to optimize reward subject to constraints on cost. Most existing safety-constrained bandit models enforce safety by requiring the expected cost of each action to remain below a prescribed threshold. However, this may be insufficient in heteroscedastic settings, where the chosen action affects not only the expected reward and cost, but also the variability of the observed outcomes. We study contextual bandits with one-dimensional continuous actions and stage-wise high-probability constraints on the realized cost. We propose High-Probability Constrained UCB, an optimistic-pessimistic algorithm that explores for reward while conservatively estimating the safe action set. For linear reward and cost models, we prove a tight $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$ regret bound, and we extend the analysis to general function classes using the eluder dimension. Experiments show that enforcing realized-cost safety substantially reduces violations compared with expected-cost constrained baselines.

</details>


### [366] [Beyond Client Averaging: A Client-Independent Second-Order Stationary-Bias Component in Stochastic SCAFFOLD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26765)
*Yi-Ping Tang,Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在全参与随机 ScaF 算法中，客户端平均虽能抑制 $O(\gamma/N)$ 的主导偏差，但无法消除由非二次曲率和梯度噪声引起的与客户端数量无关的 $O(\gamma^2)$ 稳态均值偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的常步长分析仅识别出 $O(\gamma/N)$ 的主导稳态均值偏差，并指出高阶偏差随客户端数量增加可能持续存在，但未能从系数层面识别出首个与客户端数量无关的偏差贡献项。

Method: 针对一维同质客户端、固定局部步数 $H$ 且有界加性梯度噪声的全参与随机 ScaF 设置，通过数学推导证明了稳态均值偏差的渐近展开式，分析了控制变量如何通过改变局部轨迹的二阶矩，经非二次曲率转换后生成稳态偏差。

Result: 推导出了具体的偏差公式，显示偏差包含 $O(\gamma/N)$ 项和与 $N$ 无关的 $O(\gamma^2)$ 项；当目标函数为非二次型且系数非零时，$O(\gamma^2)$ 偏差项持续存在；数值实验验证了预测系数的准确性及其随客户端数量增加的持久性。

Conclusion: 客户端平均机制是间接的，虽在线性全局平均中抵消了直接控制贡献，但控制变量仍改变了局部轨迹的二阶矩，导致在非二次曲率下产生稳态偏差；该结果目前仅限于一维同质固定 $H$ 的设置。

Abstract: Existing constant-step analysis of stochastic \Scaf{} identifies a leading $O(γ/N)$ stationary mean bias and shows that higher-order bias can persist as the client count increases, but does not identify the first client-independent contribution at coefficient level. For full-participation stochastic \Scaf{} with one-dimensional homogeneous clients, fixed local-step count $H$, and bounded additive gradient noise, we prove, uniformly over $N\ge2$,
  $$ \begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}_{π_{γ,N,H}}[x]-x^\star ={}& -\frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{4f''(x^\star)^2}\fracγ{N}\\ &- \frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{12f''(x^\star)} \frac{(H-1)(5H-1)}{H}γ^2 +O_H\!\left(\frac{γ^2}{N}+γ^3\right). \end{aligned} $$
  Hence client averaging suppresses the leading $O(γ/N)$ bias but does not remove the client-independent $O(γ^2)$ component when its coefficient is nonzero.
  The mechanism is indirect: although the direct control contribution cancels pathwise in the linear global average, the controls still alter within-round local trajectories and their second moments. Fresh gradient noise and persistent control fluctuations therefore generate local second-moment corrections that nonquadratic curvature converts into stationary mean bias. The coefficient vanishes for quadratic objectives. Numerical experiments are consistent with the predicted coefficient, its persistence as client count increases, and the stated joint remainder. The result is restricted to the one-dimensional homogeneous fixed-$H$ setting.

</details>


### [367] [On the Indistinguishability of Human v/s AI Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26797)
*Jaee Ponde,Aritra Das,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用人类写作样本，通过策略性改写将机器生成的文本分布向人类文本分布靠拢，并推导了收敛速率及样本量与改写轮次的缩放关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的快速发展使得区分AI生成文本与人类写作变得日益困难，尤其是经过旨在使机器文本更具“人性”的改写工具处理后，这一挑战更为严峻。

Method: 在针对相同提示的人类和机器回复的多样本设置下，研究分析了在简单混合和稳定性条件下，重复改写如何使机器分布向经验人类分布移动。

Result: 推导出了明确的收敛速率，将分析扩展至有限样本设置，并刻画了所需人类样本数量和改写轮次如何随期望误差进行缩放。

Conclusion: 通过利用人类写作样本进行策略性改写，可以有效地将机器生成的响应分布向人类分布收敛，且该过程具有可量化的收敛特性。

Abstract: The rapid improvement of LLMs has made distinguishing AI-generated text from human writing a pressing problem. This challenge is further amplified by paraphrasing tools designed to make machine-generated text appear more "human". We study how access to human writing samples can be used to strategically paraphrase machine-generated responses toward the human distribution. Under a multi-sample setting with human and machine responses to the same prompts, we show that repeated paraphrasing moves the machine distribution toward the empirical human distribution under simple mixing and stability conditions. Our results derive an explicit convergence rate, extend the analysis to a finite-sample setting, and characterize how the required number of human samples and paraphrasing rounds scale with the desired error.

</details>


### [368] [SAGE: Variate-Wise Semantic Augmentation for Vision-Language Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26829)
*Haizhao Fan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE 是一个基于 CLIP 的端到端框架，通过联合建模时序、跨变量、文本和视觉信息，将领域语义知识注入时间序列预测，在无需推理时大语言模型的情况下实现了最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型仅处理原始数值序列，缺乏领域专家隐含使用的语义知识（如变量物理意义、统计行为和时序动态）。现有解决方案要么推理时依赖计算昂贵的大语言模型，要么在数据集层面应用统一的文本提示，忽略了单个变量间的异质语义。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架：1) 利用 CLIP 文本编码器处理频率增强补丁和变量令牌，并通过门控残差路径注入变量特定描述和统计描述符；2) 并行使用冻结的 CLIP 视觉编码器，通过仅训练阶段的对比目标将渲染后的序列与时序表示对齐；3) 双重利用 CLIP 提供互补的语义和视觉监督，避免在预测循环中引入 LLM。

Result: 在八个长期预测基准测试和 M4 数据集上，SAGE 实现了最先进（SOTA）的预测精度。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实，多模态对齐和变量级知识的引入带来了互补的性能提升，证明了该框架有效桥接了数值序列与领域语义知识之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models operate on raw numerical sequences, lacking the semantic knowledge that domain experts implicitly leverage, such as the physical meaning of each variable, its statistical behavior, and its temporal dynamics. Recent efforts to bridge this gap fall into two camps. Some rely on large language models at inference time, which is computationally expensive. Others apply uniform textual prompts at the dataset level, ignoring the heterogeneous semantics across individual variates. We propose SAGE (Seeing and Augmenting with Grounded Encoding), an end-to-end CLIP-based framework that jointly models temporal, cross-variable, textual, and visual information. The CLIP text encoder processes frequency-enhanced patches and variable tokens, while gated residual paths inject variable-specific descriptions and statistical descriptors. In parallel, the frozen CLIP vision encoder aligns rendered series with temporal representations through a training-only contrastive objective. This dual use of CLIP adds complementary semantic and visual supervision without placing an LLM in the forecasting loop. Across eight long-term benchmarks and M4, SAGE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. Ablations confirm complementary gains from multimodal alignment and variable-level knowledge.

</details>


### [369] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Control of CAV Platoon Joining Maneuvers in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26860)
*Biao Yin,Abderrahmane Kasmi,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于研究网联自动驾驶车辆（CAV）编队加入行为的通用建模与仿真框架，比较了DQN、DDQN和PPO三种深度强化学习算法在混合交通环境中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV编队控制在实际交通中面临不确定性和异构驾驶行为的挑战，强化学习虽有潜力但存在安全性和学习效率问题，需要研究如何在混合交通环境中实现安全高效的编队加入。

Method: 采用基于智能体的建模框架与SUMO仿真器结合，评估DQN、DDQN和PPO算法；通过在奖励函数中加入风险惩罚或使用外部安全控制器来约束学习策略。

Result: PPO表现最优，加入成功率约98%，碰撞率低于1%，但需要更多决策步骤；外部安全控制器可有效防止碰撞但可能降低加入效率。

Conclusion: 在设计混合交通中基于RL的CAV编队加入控制器时，需要同时考虑安全性和效率，二者之间存在权衡关系。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicle (CAV) platooning offers a promising approach to improving road safety and traffic capacity. However, platoon control in real-world traffic is challenging due to uncertainty and heterogeneous driving behaviors. Reinforcement learning (RL) has strong potential for addressing such control problems, but its practical deployment raises challenges related to safety and learning efficiency. This paper proposes a generic modeling and simulation framework for investigating CAV platoon joining maneuvers and comparing deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based control algorithms. The problem is particularly challenging in mixed-traffic environments, where CAVs coexist with human-driven vehicles exhibiting heterogeneous longitudinal and lateral behaviors. The objective is to achieve safe and efficient joining maneuvers by either incorporating penalties for risky behaviors into the learning process or using an external safety controller to constrain the learned policy. An agent-based modeling framework coupled with the Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) simulator is used to evaluate Deep Q-Network (DQN), Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN), and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Results show that PPO outperforms DQN and DDQN, achieving a joining success rate of approximately 98 % and a collision rate below 1 %, largely due to risk-related penalties incorporated into the reward function. However, this improved performance requires more decision steps to complete the maneuver, revealing a trade-off between safety, joining effectiveness, and decision efficiency. An external safety controller effectively prevents collisions, although its interventions may reduce joining efficiency. The results highlight the importance of jointly considering safety and efficiency when designing RL-based controllers for CAV platoon joining in mixed traffic.

</details>


### [370] [Mitigating Strong-Modality Collapse in Multimodal Learning via Inverted Asymmetric Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26879)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态融合中主导模态性能下降（强模态坍塌）的问题，提出反转非对称融合（IAF）方法，通过保留主导模态原样并让弱模态对其进行关注，结合模态感知知识蒸馏，在多个基准测试中显著优于单模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态融合模型在部分数据集（如MultiHuSE）上无法超越最佳单模态基线的问题，特别是针对融合过程中主导模态（如文本）准确率大幅下降的“强模态坍塌”现象。

Method: 提出反转非对称融合（IAF）架构：1. 避免模态间强制相互关注，保持主导模态通路不变以作为上下文锚点；2. 让较弱模态主动关注主导模态；3. 在融合前利用模态感知知识蒸馏（Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation）增强较弱模态的表征能力。

Result: 在MultiHuSE、UR-FUNNY（文本主导）和MUStARD（音视频主导）三个基准测试中，IAF不仅将主导模态的内部准确率维持在其单模态上限，避免了传统对称融合高达18.5%的性能下降，且相比最强单模态基线提升了最高8.25%。

Conclusion: IAF有效解决了强模态坍塌问题，证明了在非对称融合策略中保护主导模态完整性并增强弱模态的重要性，为多模态模型超越单模态基线提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Fusing multiple modalities is expected to improve model performance. However, on the MultiHuSE dataset, early, late, and symmetric attention fusion often fail to outperform the best unimodal baseline (text). Pathway isolation of a symmetric attention fusion model reveals that the text-pathway accuracy drops from 74.9% to 56.4% after fusion in one such setting, indicating that the dominant modality can be degraded during integration. We term this strong-modality collapse and argue that it helps explain why some multimodal models fail to surpass unimodal baselines. We propose Inverted Asymmetric Fusion (IAF), which avoids forcing mutual attention across modalities. The dominant modality is preserved by passing through fusion unchanged, while weaker modalities attend to it as a contextual anchor. Before fusion, weaker modalities are strengthened using Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation. We evaluate IAF on three benchmarks with different modality hierarchies: text-dominant datasets (MultiHuSE, UR-FUNNY) and an audio-visual-dominant dataset (MUStARD). Pathway isolation shows that IAF preserves the dominant modality's internal accuracy at its unimodal ceiling across all tested configurations, whereas symmetric fusion degrades it by up to 18.5% on MultiHuSE. IAF improves over the strongest unimodal baseline by up to 8.25%.

</details>


### [371] [A Layer Importance Metric for Quantization Accounting for the Speed-Quality Trade-off in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26926)
*Artem Safronov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合信息保留率和吞吐量增益的复合指标，用于优化小语言模型在资源受限设备上的量化策略，实现速度与质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在内存和计算资源受限的设备上部署时，自回归推理受内存带宽限制，统一量化会损害模型性能，因为架构冗余有限且仅少数层对低精度不敏感。

Method: 提出复合指标，结合基于归一化 SQNR 的信息保留率和基于 roofline 模型的吞吐量增益，通过剖析 Gemma 3 1B 模型确定 FFN 块和嵌入矩阵为加速目标，无需实际执行即可估算质量和速度得分。

Result: 在多个模型架构上评估，加速速度预测误差约为 4%；相比进化搜索、专用加速器或基于 Shapley 值的方法，该方法能将更多资源分配给最具表达力的层。

Conclusion: 该分析方法使小语言模型量化成为可预测的工程任务，提供了一种通用且高效的量化优先级排序策略。

Abstract: Small language models (sLLMs) are nowadays hosted on devices with limited memory and computational budget. In an autoregressive setup, inference is memory-bandwidth bound: uniform quantization is often detrimental to such models, since their architecture has limited redundancies and only a few layers are not very sensitive to lower precision. We propose a composite metric that combines two orthogonal criteria: information retention (measured in terms of a normalized SQNR-based coefficient) and throughput gains (modeled using a roofline-based latency analysis). By profiling Gemma 3 1B, we find that Feed-Forward Network blocks and the embedding matrix are the most promising targets for acceleration. For each candidate, we estimate a normalized quality score based on simulated quantization and a normalized speed score based on roofline modeling with no actual execution needed. We combine the two scores in a composite priority coefficient, allowing us to tune the trade-off between speed and quality as needed. Our metric is general and can be used to prioritize individual blocks, their projection sublayers, or transformer layers as a whole. We evaluate our approach on several model architectures, showing that our estimates have at around 4% prediction error for the accelerated speedup. We find that our method generally allocates more resources to the most expressive layers compared to evolutionary search, specialized accelerators, or Shapley-value-based approaches that require expensive approximate inference. Our analytical approach makes sLLM quantization a predictable engineering task.

</details>


### [372] [Scaling Model-Generated Distillation Data Can Make Latent Teacher Traits More Recoverable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26958)
*Zhichen Dong,Zhixuan Liu,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Shuyang Zhang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模型生成数据扩展的“第二效应”：即使在离任（off-task）数据中，扩大训练规模也会放大学生模型对教师特定隐含特质（trait）的继承，而非单纯提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为扩展模型生成数据仅能通过增加覆盖率和减少噪声来优化蒸馏效果；然而，作者旨在探究数据规模是否会在无意中增强学生对教师微妙特质信号的检测与模仿，即便这些数据表面看来与目标特质无关。

Method: 采用受潜意识学习启发的受控实验设置：诱导教师模型表达特定特质并生成受限的离任数据（如仅数字补全）；训练不同数据规模的学生模型，并在独立领域通过匹配的无特质对照组评估其特质迁移情况；同时分析学到的LoRA更新以验证趋势。

Result: 研究发现，更大的独立数据集使教师的诱导特质在学生行为中更加显著。若小规模学生已偏向该特质，扩展主要起放大作用；若偏向其他相关特质，增加数据可将其行为转向预期特质。该效应在不同模型族、特质类型、多特质设置及跨模型迁移中均普遍存在。

Conclusion: 扩展生成的蒸馏数据必须与感知特质的筛选（curation）和评估相结合，因为即使数据看似离任或良性，规模化仍会意外地强化教师模型中特定的、潜在的行为特质。

Abstract: Scaling model-generated data is usually viewed as improving distillation: more examples should increase coverage, reduce noise, and produce stronger students. We show a second effect: larger datasets can make subtle teacher-specific signals easier to detect in the trained student, even when examples are off-task and never mention the trait. In a controlled setup inspired by subliminal learning, a teacher induced to express a target trait generates restricted off-task data, such as number-only completions. Students trained on different amounts of independent off-task data are evaluated in a separate domain, with matched no-trait controls isolating target-specific transfer. Our main finding is that larger independent datasets make the teacher's induced trait stand out more clearly in the student's later behavior. Other plausible traits may also strengthen with scale, but the target usually grows more. When the small-scale student already favors the target, scaling mainly amplifies that behavior; when it favors a related or salient alternative, more data can shift behavior toward the intended trait. Analyses of learned LoRA updates show a parallel trend. These effects appear across model families, trait types, multi-trait settings, and cross-model transfer. Our results suggest that scaling generated distillation data should be paired with trait-aware curation and evaluation, even when the data appears off-task or benign.

</details>


### [373] [Packora: Systematic Design for Generative Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26962)
*Nayoung Kim,Kiyoung Seong,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Packora，一种基于流的生成模型，用于分子晶体结构预测（CSP）。该模型能从分子图联合预测原子坐标和晶格，支持多组分及有机金属晶体，并在生成和排序基准测试中均优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体结构预测（CSP）在制药、农化和有机电子领域至关重要，因为分子构象和堆积的细微差异会显著影响材料性质。现有的方法在联合预测原子坐标与晶格、处理复杂晶体系统（如多组分和有机金属）以及灵活利用约束条件方面存在局限，需要更强大且通用的生成模型。

Method: 提出 Packora，一种基于流（flow-based）的生成模型。其核心方法包括：1) 从分子图联合生成原子坐标和晶格参数；2) 支持多组分和有机金属晶体；3) 可在单一模型中灵活条件化分子构象子集、立体化学标签和空间群信息；4) 采用可缓存的成对推理机制、特定的训练目标、数值求解器选择、条件化 Dropout 以及成对与单一表示的平衡缩放策略进行架构优化。评估策略上，受 CCDC CSP 盲测启发，将生成质量与排序性能分开评估。

Result: Packora 在结构生成和排序基准测试中均超越了基线模型。具体表现为：在所有六个生成基准测试中实现了最佳匹配预算覆盖率（matched-budget coverage）；在排序任务中，实现了更高的实验结构恢复率、更低的实验结构排名以及更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: Packora 通过创新的架构设计和训练策略，显著提升了分子晶体结构预测的生成质量和排序性能，为制药和材料科学领域提供了一种高效、通用且高精度的 CSP 解决方案。

Abstract: Molecular crystal structure prediction (CSP) is important in pharmaceuticals, agrochemicals, and organic electronics, where subtle differences in molecular conformation and packing can strongly affect material properties. We present Packora, a flow-based generative model for molecular CSP that jointly predicts atomic coordinates and the lattice from molecular graphs. Packora supports multi-component and organometallic crystals and can condition on any subset of molecular conformers, stereochemical labels, and space-group information within a single model. Inspired by the CCDC CSP blind test, we evaluate generation and ranking separately, using generation to isolate generator quality and ranking to measure end-to-end performance under a common relaxation and ranking pipeline. We also systematically study architecture, training, conditioning, inference, and scaling, identifying an effective design based on cacheable pairwise reasoning, training objective and numerical solver choices, conditioning dropout, and balanced scaling of pairwise and single representations. Packora outperforms the baselines on both structure generation and ranking benchmarks, achieving the best matched-budget coverage across all six generation benchmarks, as well as higher experimental-form recovery, lower experimental-form ranks, and faster convergence in ranking.

</details>


### [374] [Adversarial Training Without Input Gradients via Low-Rank Householder Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26963)
*Tiana C. Johnson,Donsub Rim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低秩Householder展开（LRHE）的对抗训练方法，无需对输入进行梯度迭代即可生成小范数对抗样本，显著降低了训练成本，同时在特定扰动范围内达到了与传统PGD方法相当的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统对抗训练（如PGD）中内层最大化搜索计算成本高昂的问题，并探索是否可以在不对输入进行微分的情况下，利用训练好的深度神经网络固有的输入不稳定性来生成有效的对抗样本。

Method: 提出低秩Householder展开（LRHE）技术，通过线性化描述组合仿射映射，直接从网络参数和正向传播中的激活模式中计算对抗方向，无需输入梯度迭代；在此基础上构建一种仅需额外正向评估和标准反向传播的对抗训练方案，完全消除了传统的内层最大化搜索。

Result: 该方法的正则化成本相当于每epoch 2.8个PGD步骤，相比40步对抗训练成本降低了8.7倍，甚至低于3步训练的成本；在相对$\ell^2$预算$\varepsilon \le 0.02$时，其模型表现与3步PGD训练相当，在$\varepsilon \le 0.012$时与40步PGD训练相当。

Conclusion: 证明了无需对输入微分即可构建有效的对抗正则化器，利用LRHE生成的局部几何信息足以在小范数扰动范围内实现高效的对抗训练，且计算效率显著提升，但效果受限于展开的局部性，在大扰动下性能下降。

Abstract: This work concerns adversarial training against the small-norm adversarial examples that arise from the inherent input instability of a trained deep neural network. Examples in this class are small as measured in the relative $\ell^2$-norm, and therefore lie in the neighborhood of the input on which the model acts approximately linearly, the regime in which the perturbation remains imperceptible. We first show that such examples can be computed directly from the trained network parameters, without input gradient iterations, by means of a linearization called the low-rank Householder expansion (LRHE). The expansion describes the composed affine map rather than any individual layer, and the directions it identifies are read from the activation pattern already available in the forward pass. We then propose a simple adversarial training scheme built on this construction. No differentiation with respect to the input is performed at any point: training requires only additional forward evaluations, with weight parameters updated by the standard backward pass, and the inner maximization of the usual min-max formulation is eliminated entirely. That such a regularizer exists is our main finding: the methods that dispense with the inner search all obtain their local geometry by differentiating with respect to the input, and we show this is not necessary. The regularizer costs the equivalent of $2.8$ PGD steps per epoch, an $8.7\times$ reduction relative to 40-step adversarial training on MNIST and below the cost of 3-step training. The resulting models match three-step PGD adversarial training for relative $\ell^2$ budgets $\varepsilon \le 0.02$ and 40-step training for $\varepsilon \le 0.012$, falling away beyond, consistent with the locality of the expansion.

</details>


### [375] [Graph-Based Pseudo-multimodal Contrastive Learning for 12-Lead ECG Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26964)
*Mengyu Wang,Kozo Okada,Takafumi Goto,Natsuko Jinba,Hiroki Yamaya,Kiyoshi Hibi,Tomoki Hamagami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的伪多模态对比学习框架Graph-CMMC，用于12导联心电图分析，通过GADF图像构建互补表示，并利用图模块建模导联间依赖关系，在冠状动脉闭塞分类任务中取得了与监督学习方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的心电图分析方法大多关注单导联信号或将各导联独立处理，通常将心电图信号作为一维时间序列数据使用CNN或RNN处理，难以捕捉临床诊断所必需的导联间依赖性和全局波形模式。

Method: 提出Graph-CMMC框架，将心电图波形转换为GADF图像构建伪多模态学习环境，采用自监督方式对齐波形和GADF表示，同时使用基于图的关系模块在对比学习过程中建模导联间依赖关系并强制导联间结构一致性。

Result: 在多标签冠状动脉闭塞分类任务上的实验结果表明，该框架取得了与监督学习方法相当的性能，验证了使用GADF作为互补表示以及引入显式图建模导联间依赖关系的有效性。

Conclusion: Graph-CMMC框架通过GADF互补表示和图结构建模有效学习了鲁棒的12导联心电图表示，为心电图分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: 12-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) is a standard, non-invasive examination widely used for diagnosing coronary artery disease, where clinical interpretation relies on comparing waveform patterns across multiple leads. However, most existing ECG analysis methods focus on single-lead signals or treat each lead independently, and typically process ECG signals as one-dimensional time-series data using CNNs or RNNs. While effective in modeling local waveform changes, such approaches have difficulty capturing inter-lead dependency and global waveform patterns essential for clinical diagnosis.
  To address this limitation, we propose a graph-based pseudo-multimodal contrastive learning framework called Graph-CMMC. ECG waveforms are transformed into Gramian Angular Difference Field (GADF) images to construct complementary representations of the same cardiac activity, enabling a pseudo-multimodal learning setting. Using all 12 leads, Graph-CMMC aligns waveform and GADF representations in a self-supervised manner, while a graph-based relational module is employed to model inter-lead dependency and enforce structural consistency across leads during contrastive learning.
  Experimental results on a multi-label coronary artery occlusion classification task demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance compared to supervised learning methods. These results further suggest the effectiveness of using GADF as a complementary representation and incorporating explicit graph-based modeling of inter-lead dependency for learning robust 12-lead ECG representations.

</details>


### [376] [ClusterAttention: A training-free speedup of bidirectional attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26965)
*Kasper Nordenram,Amelie Dittmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 ClusterAttention 的通用、无需训练的双向注意力层加速方法，通过快速递归聚类适应键值几何结构，实现任意簇大小设置，并在保持高精度的同时显著提升计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法要么依赖输入结构（如语言顺序或图像空间邻近性），要么使用在多次前向传播中摊销的慢速聚类过程，缺乏在结构化输入和单次前向传播场景下的高效、无需训练的通用加速方案。

Method: 采用快速递归聚类方法，根据每个注意力头中键和查询的几何结构生成有用簇，并将所有簇设置为固定大小（2 的幂次），使块稀疏注意力在 GPU 上的延迟与密集注意力相同；同时推导稀疏注意力输出误差表达式，并通过质心补偿机制优化误差。

Result: 在大规模表格数据上，ClusterAttention 将 TabPFN-3 加速 2-6 倍，保留至少 99% 的密集注意力精度；在视频生成任务中，相比 SVOO 方法实现更高精度和更大加速比（1.8x vs 1.4x），且无需离线校准。

Conclusion: ClusterAttention 是首个可在结构化输入和单次前向传播场景中成功应用的无需训练方法，通过自适应聚类和误差补偿机制，在保持高精度的同时显著提升注意力计算效率，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces ClusterAttention, a general training-free speedup of bidirectional attention layers. Existing sparse attention methods either rely on structure in the input, such as order in language or spatial proximity in images, or use slow clustering processes amortized over several forward passes. ClusterAttention instead uses a fast recursive clustering method that adapts to the geometry of the keys and queries in each attention head to produce useful clusters. This method allows setting the size of the clusters arbitrarily. We utilize this by setting all clusters to be a fixed size that is a power of two, allowing the block-sparse attention to run at the same latency per query-key interaction as dense attention on GPUs. We also derive an expression for the output error in sparse attention, that explains the counterintuitive experimental finding that tight clusters can lead to larger errors than random clusters. We then derive the error when excluded clusters are compensated through their centroids, and show that this error shrinks with tighter clusters. We integrate this compensation into the method.
  On large-scale tabular data ClusterAttention speeds up TabPFN-3 arXiv:2605.13986 by two to six times, while retaining at least 99% of the dense accuracy. To our knowledge, it is the first training-free method that can be successfully applied in the setting of unstructured input and a single forward pass. For video generation with Wan 2.1-14B T2V arXiv:2503.20314 , ClusterAttention achieves output closer to dense attention and a larger speedup (1.8x versus 1.4x) compared to SVOO arXiv:2603.18636 , a leading method developed specifically for this domain, both run without offline calibration.

</details>


### [377] [TEMPLAR Wales: A georeferenced environmental and toponymic dataset of Welsh settlements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26970)
*Oktay Karakuş,Can Eyupoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为TEMPLAR Wales的地理参考环境-地名数据集，包含威尔士3,757个定居点记录，将可复现的定居点框架与确定性词汇筛选及定居点级环境属性通过稳定标识符链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地名提供了景观描述和组织的持久记录，但其定量重用需要明确区分映射地点、词汇注释和环境测量。现有研究缺乏将地名与环境属性系统关联的可复用数据集。

Method: 构建包含3,757个定居点记录的地理参考数据集，使用24个威尔士地名元素的冻结注册表生成1,350个词汇检测，覆盖1,294个定居点，同时保留精确和前缀标记匹配及其来源。数据集包含河流和海岸接近度、海拔、局部地形背景、土地覆盖和邻近木本覆盖等环境属性，并采用独立高程产品进行并行地形测量。

Result: 技术验证确认了关系完整性、确定性词汇重建、记录的环境覆盖范围、独立地形来源之间的强一致性以及冻结版本的可复现重建。数据集以四个关系表形式分发，附带字段级数据字典、来源注册表和许可元数据。

Conclusion: TEMPLAR Wales为地名学、语言地理学、历史和环境景观研究、GIS及空间数据分析提供了可复用的基础，但明确指出计算词汇检测不应被视为已验证的词源，当代环境测量也不应视为历史景观重建。

Abstract: Place names provide persistent records of how landscapes have been described and organised, but their quantitative reuse requires explicit separation between mapped places, lexical annotations and environmental measurements. TEMPLAR Wales is a georeferenced environmental-toponymy dataset comprising 3,757 settlement records across Wales. The resource links a reproducible settlement frame to deterministic lexical screening and settlement-level environmental attributes through stable identifiers. It contains 1,350 lexical detections across 1,294 settlements, generated from a frozen registry of 24 Welsh place-name elements, while retaining exact- and prefix-token matches and their provenance separately. Environmental attributes describe river and coastal proximity, elevation and local terrain context at multiple spatial scales, land cover and neighbourhood woody cover, with parallel terrain measurements derived from independent elevation products. The dataset is distributed as four relational tables accompanied by a field-level data dictionary, source-provenance register and licensing metadata. Technical validation confirms relational integrity, deterministic lexical reconstruction, documented environmental coverage, strong agreement between independent terrain sources and reproducible reconstruction of the frozen release. TEMPLAR Wales provides a reusable foundation for research in toponymy, linguistic geography, historical and environmental landscape studies, GIS and spatial data analysis without treating computational lexical detections as verified etymologies or contemporary environmental measurements as historical landscape reconstructions.

</details>


### [378] [Terrain signatures in Welsh settlement names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26978)
*Oktay Karakuş,Can Eyupoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 威尔士定居点名称（特别是高地形和低地形词汇）保留了可测量的现代地形信息，高地形名称所在位置比周围环境平均高出约24米，将地形名称极性加入空间模型可显著降低预测误差，但研究未证实命名因果关系或历史环境记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究景观名称是否除了反映宏观地理结构外，还保留了可测量的具体环境信息，这一假设此前很少经过严格测试。

Method: 分析3,757个威尔士定居点，使用冻结的、来源审核的24元素词汇框架；采用预注册的特定结果模型和地理结构化验证；核心对比携带高地形元素（bryn/mynydd）与低地形元素（cwm/pant）的定居点，并在不同邻域定义及独立高程数据源中进行复现验证。

Result: 高地形名称所在位置比其2公里范围内环境平均高出24.4米（p=0.00137），该关联在不同邻域定义及独立数据源中保持一致；在空间基线模型中加入地形名称极性后，地理留出的均方误差降低了4.63%-7.30%；河流相关名称证据较弱，林地模型无法估算。

Conclusion: 选定的威尔士定居点名称类别保留了关于当前地形的可测量信息，但研究局限在于残留的空间结构、未解析的命名语言及缺乏独立外部复现，因此不能确立个体词源、命名因果关系、历史环境记忆或对其他命名系统的可转移性。

Abstract: Landscapes are named, but whether names retain measurable environmental information beyond broad geographic structure is rarely tested. We analysed 3,757 Welsh settlements using a frozen, source-audited 24-element lexical framework, preregistered outcome-specific models and geographically structured validation. The central comparison contrasted 101 settlements carrying high-terrain elements (\textit{bryn} or \textit{mynydd}) with 139 carrying low-terrain elements (\textit{cwm} or \textit{pant}). High-terrain names occupied locations 24.4 m higher relative to their 2-km surroundings (95\% CI, 10.8--38.1 m; Holm-adjusted $p$ = 0.00137). The association remained positive across prespecified 1-, 2- and 5-km neighbourhood definitions and was reproduced using an independently produced elevation source (24.1 m; 95\% CI, 10.6--37.6 m). Adding terrain-name polarity to a non-lexical spatial and settlement baseline reduced geographically held-out mean squared error by 4.63\%, 6.22\% and 7.30\% under 10-, 25- and 50-km spatial blocking, respectively, although improvement varied among held-out regions. River-related names provided weaker, directionally consistent evidence, while the preregistered woodland model was non-estimable. Residual spatial structure, unresolved name language and the absence of independent external replication limit interpretation. Selected Welsh settlement-name categories therefore retain measurable information about present-day terrain within Wales, without establishing individual etymology, causal naming, historical environmental memory or transferability to other naming systems.

</details>


### [379] [Decentralized Multitask Learning over Learned Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26989)
*Zirui Wan,Stefan Vlaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化两阶段多任务学习策略，在未知任务关系的情况下，先从噪声梯度中估计图拉普拉斯矩阵，再利用学习到的图进行协作扩散学习，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图正则化多任务学习框架通常假设任务关系结构已知，但实际场景中任务间的依赖关系往往是未知的，需要从分布式数据中直接学习。

Method: 提出去中心化两阶段策略：第一阶段基于高斯马尔可夫随机场先验，从噪声非合作随机梯度迭代中估计广义图拉普拉斯矩阵；第二阶段利用学习到的图实现协作多任务扩散学习。

Result: 理论分析量化了拉普拉斯估计误差及其对多任务扩散递归稳态性能的影响，并引入拓扑敏感度指数刻画网络异质性影响；仿真表明学习到的任务图使协作学习性能显著优于非协作学习，且在估计步长足够小时接近真实图基准。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了未知任务关系下的去中心化多任务学习问题，通过估计任务图实现协作学习，提升了整体性能，理论分析与实验结果一致。

Abstract: This paper investigates decentralized multitask learning over networks when the underlying task relationships are unknown. While existing graph-regularized multitask frameworks typically assume a known structure, practical settings often require learning inter-task dependencies directly from distributed data. We propose a decentralized two-phase strategy that first estimates a generalized graph Laplacian from noisy non-cooperative stochastic gradient iterates, and subsequently exploits the learned graph to enable cooperative multitask diffusion learning. This framework is motivated by a Gaussian Markov random field prior, which gives rise to a decentralized maximum likelihood estimator for the graph Laplacian. The analysis quantifies the Laplacian estimation error and its propagation to the steady-state performance of the multitask diffusion recursion, and introduces a topology sensitivity index to capture the effect of network heterogeneity. Simulation results corroborate the theoretical findings and demonstrate that cooperation enabled by the learned task graph significantly improves performance over non-cooperative learning, while approaching the true-graph baseline when the estimation stepsize is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [380] [Benchmarking_Fast_Domain_Adaptation_for_Unsupervised_Speech_Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26992)
*Robin San Roman,Manel Khentout,Tu Anh Nguyen,Paul Michel,Yossi Adi,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ABX-Accent基准，用于评估表示学习方法在不同英语口音上的表现，并展示了一种自适应领域归一化方法，在跨说话人ABX评分上平均提升了23.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示学习在语音建模中表现良好，但对于域外语音（特别是口音多样的长尾分布）的处理能力及小样本适应能力研究不足。

Method: 基于AESRC数据集构建ABX-Accent基准（包含10种英语口音，每种口音<10小时无标签训练数据），并采用自适应领域归一化微调预训练的对比预测编码（CPC）模型。

Result: 在ABX-Accent基准上，该方法相比未适配模型，跨说话人ABX评分平均相对提升23.6%。

Conclusion: ABX-Accent基准和自适应领域归一化方法有效提升了模型对口音变化的适应能力，相关数据和指标将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Representation learning has attracted great atten- tion and managed to reach good performances as a pretraining method for downstream tasks or as a first step towards unsu- pervised speech modeling. Yet, little is known about how such methods deal with out-of-domain speech and how could they be adapted in a few shot to new domains. This is important especially for accented speech where one observes a long tail of accents that diverge from the standard ones. We introduce ABX- Accent, a benchmark based on the AESRC dataset that features 10 different accents of English. It includes a small (< 10 hours) unlabelled training set in each of the accents and adaptations of the Zero Resources Challenge ABX evaluation metrics to each of the accents. We illustrate this benchmark with a baseline model that uses adaptive domain normalization to fine tune a pretrained Contrastive Predictive Coding model on the accents. This method is first developed on LibriSpeech using a male/female split. When applied to the new benchmark, the proposed method yields a relative improvement of 23.6% on across-speaker ABX scores on average compared to non adapted models. The data and metrics will be open sourced upon paper acceptance

</details>


### [381] [Disentangling Optimization Scale from Preference Scale in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27032)
*Ivan Kruzhilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了DPO中β参数的双重作用，并提出了一种新的中心化softplus重构方法，使参数可独立调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO中的β参数同时控制偏好噪声尺度和优化动力学，导致策略偏差非单调、损失值不可比、超参数敏感等问题。

Method: 提出中心化softplus重构，使逆偏好噪声尺度和学习率效应显式化且可独立调节，并在β→0时退化为线性偏好边际目标。

Result: 新方法解决了β参数纠缠问题，使优化过程更稳定，超参数调节更简单，且保持了与原始DPO的argmin等价性。

Conclusion: 该重构方法提高了DPO的可解释性和可调性，为语言模型对齐提供了更优的优化目标。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used objective for aligning language models from preference data, with the coefficient $β$ commonly interpreted as controlling the KL constraint to a reference policy. We show that $β$ entangles two distinct roles: it governs the effective inverse preference-noise scale and simultaneously rescales the optimization dynamics, coupling this scale with the effective step size. As a consequence, at a fixed learning rate the achieved policy deviation is non-monotone in $β$: it vanishes in a dead zone at small $β$, reaches a peak at an intermediate value, and decreases again for larger $β$. Moreover, standard DPO loss values are not comparable across $β$: runs with nearly identical loss curves can differ several-fold in KL divergence from the reference model. This entanglement obscures the role of $β$, increases sensitivity to hyperparameter choices, and complicates learning-rate scheduling. We propose a centered-softplus reformulation that is argmin-equivalent to DPO for $β>0$, while making the inverse preference-noise-scale and learning-rate effects explicit and independently tunable. The normalized centered-softplus objective also admits a continuous $β\to0$ endpoint that reduces to a linear preference-margin objective.

</details>


### [382] [Performance Foundations of Parallel & Distributed Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27046)
*Maciej Besta,Leonard Schmidt,Lara Nonino,Robert Gerstenberger,Pierre Pang,Patrik Okanovic,Ales Kubicek,Tiancheng Chen,Baraq Lipshitz,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了推理语言模型（RLMs）训练的计算挑战，系统化了RL-for-LLM范式，提出了并行策略分类，并为构建可扩展、高效、低成本的RLMs提供了实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLM训练需要数百万GPU小时和复杂的多模型流水线，计算成本极高，使其成为并行分布式系统问题，需要系统性优化以降低训练成本并提高效率。

Method: 1. 系统化RL-for-LLM范式并进行以计算为中心的分析；2. 建立RLM训练的内/间模型并行策略分类体系；3. 利用并行计算的工作深度模型使分类严谨可移植；4. 分析现有框架并提炼实践指南。

Result: 提出了涵盖传统和新型并行技术的完整分类体系，包括数据/张量/流水线/序列/上下文/专家并行，以及分离式放置、阶段融合、混合并行和异步执行等新技术。

Conclusion: 为构建可扩展、快速且成本效益高的RLMs提供了实践指南，并指出了开放的研究方向，有助于推动RLM训练的系统优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and other RL-style post-training paradigms have been used for aligning large language models (LLMs) with reasoning standards. The resulting recent Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) such as DeepSeek-R1, o3, and Kimi k1.5 show that such RL-style post-training ("RL-for-LLMs") can substantially improve chain-of-thought reasoning, long-horizon planning, and self-correction. However, the computational footprint of these systems is massive: state-of-the-art RLM training requires millions of GPU-hours and tightly coupled multi-model pipelines that stress modern hardware far beyond classical supervised LLM training. This makes RLM training as much a parallel and distributed systems problem as an algorithmic one. In this work, to facilitate developing RLMs that are simultaneously high-performance, scalable, and cost-effective, we first systematize the RL-for-LLM paradigm and provide a compute-centric analysis of prominent post-training algorithmic frameworks: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), as well as their variants. Second, we develop a taxonomy of intra- and inter-model parallelism strategies for RL-for-LLMs, covering both traditional techniques (data, tensor, pipeline, sequence, context, and expert parallelism) as well as novel forms of parallelism and optimization techniques for multi-model RLM training, for example disaggregated placement, stage fusion, hybrid parallelism, and asynchronous execution. We harness the work-depth model of parallel computing to make our taxonomy and its insights rigorous and portable. Finally, we analyze existing RLM frameworks and we distill practical guidelines and outline open research directions for building scalable, fast, and cost-effective RLMs.

</details>


### [383] [Soft Active Electromyography Interface for Machine Learning-Enabled Silent Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27048)
*Yuta Kurotaki,Shusuke Yamakoshi,Reitaro Yoshida,Yutaka Isoda,Tamami Takano,Yuji Isano,Yusuke Miyake,Kentaro Kuribayashi,Hiroki Ota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于手部穿戴式柔性肌电（EMG）接口的无声语音识别系统，通过指尖电极靠近嘴唇采集信号，结合深度学习实现高准确率词汇分类，并验证了其在噪声和隐私敏感环境中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无声语音识别方法需要持续的面部附着设备，存在隐私担忧和信号采集不稳定的问题，因此需要一种更灵活、稳定且保护隐私的替代方案。

Method: 开发了一种软性主动肌电接口，采用液态金属互连、透明柔性印刷电路电极和弹性体封装，集成于手部穿戴设备，通过指尖电极在需要时靠近嘴唇采集EMG信号，并使用深度神经网络进行词级分类。

Result: 在30词词汇表上，对三名受试者的分类平均准确率达到97.2±1.3%，并通过实时无人机控制验证了系统在噪声和隐私敏感环境中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了软性可穿戴肌电系统作为安全、直观的人机交互界面的潜力，为无声语音识别提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Silent speech recognition (SSR) provides an alternative communication pathway in the absence of audible speech. However, conventional approaches are limited by the need for constant facial attachment, privacy concerns, and unstable signal acquisition. Here, we propose a soft, active electromyography (EMG) interface that enables word-level SSR using machine learning. Worn on the hand, the device uses a fingertip electrode that can be positioned near the lips to acquire EMG signals only when needed. The interface integrates liquid metal (LM) interconnects, transparent flexible printed circuit (FPC) electrodes, and elastomer encapsulation to ensure high mechanical stability during finger motion. A deep neural network trained on these stable signals achieved a mean accuracy of 97.2 $\pm$ 1.3% across three subjects in classifying a 30-word vocabulary, demonstrating robust linguistic discrimination. Furthermore, real-time drone control validates the practicality of this approach in noisy and privacy-sensitive environments where conventional voice recognition fails. This study highlights the potential of soft, wearable EMG systems as secure and intuitive human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [384] [Unifying Detection and Adaptation in Task-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27070)
*Dezheng Han,Anbang Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiUni框架，利用Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）的K-FAC近似主空间正交性，在无明确任务边界的场景下实现批量级任务检测与参数高效的持续学习，动态平衡知识共享与任务隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型持续学习方法通常依赖明确的任务边界进行参数约束或引入特定模块，难以适用于现实中的无任务边界（task-free）场景，且面临灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出FiUni框架：1) 基于少量下游样本估算预训练模型FIM的K-FAC近似，利用其主空间正交性反映任务相似度；2) 构建FIM导出的冻结子空间指导低秩适应（LoRA）；3) 将 incoming batch window 的Fisher主空间与历史子空间匹配，自适应决定复用、扩展或新建子空间。

Result: 实验表明，FiUni能有效推断潜在的批量级任务归属，在参数量更少的情况下，性能可与先进的任务感知持续学习方法相媲美。

Conclusion: FiUni通过利用Fisher信息矩阵特性，成功在无任务边界场景下实现了动态的知识共享与任务隔离平衡，为大语言模型的持续学习提供了高效且适用的新范式。

Abstract: To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in downstream continual learning (CL) for large language models (LLMs), existing methods typically constrain parameter updates or introduce task-specific adaptation modules. However, these methods often rely on explicit task boundaries during training, limiting their applicability to realistic task-free scenarios. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{Fi}sher-guided \textbf{uni}fied (\textbf{FiUni}) framework for batch-level task detection and parameter-efficient continual adaptation. FiUni is motivated by a key observation about the Fisher information matrix (FIM) of pre-trained models: the orthogonality among the principal subspaces of its Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) approximation, estimated from a small number of downstream task samples, can reflect the similarity between different tasks. Based on this observation, FiUni constructs FIM-derived frozen subspaces to guide low-rank adaptation (LoRA), while matching the Fisher principal subspace of each incoming batch window with historical subspaces. This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [385] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的“涌现情感偏好”框架，使智能体能根据高层目标自主调节低层目标的优先级，在多变环境中优于固定偏好策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体决策中的核心问题：在外部环境变化和内部状态演变下，竞争性的低层目标优先级是否可由高层目标自主生成的情感偏好来动态调节，而非依赖外部预设。

Method: 提出“涌现情感偏好”概念，构建包含多目标强化学习内控制器和外部偏好生成器的框架。内控制器提供基于偏好的目标导向行为库，外生成器通过强化学习将当前状态映射为针对高层目标的最优偏好分布。

Result: 实验表明，学习到的偏好函数具有情境优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持久性特征；理论推导了偏好调节诱导的策略空间及最优性差距上界，证明当最优策略可被行为库表示时，该差距消失。

Conclusion: 通过优化涌现的情感偏好机制，智能体可自主实现动态目标优先级调节，该计算模型在多变环境中显著优于固定偏好和手工设计偏好策略，为情感在决策中的功能角色提供了计算实现路径。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [386] [Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27076)
*Joshua Le Grice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对市场状态稳健性的超参数选择方法，结合XGBoost和TabNet构建混合集成模型，在美股大盘股上实现年化收益51.26%、夏普比率2.44、CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011）的显著超额收益，且收益主要来自选股能力而非市场暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有股票预测模型评估未显式针对市场状态（regime）稳健性进行超参数选择，而算法交易市场中即使信号稳健性的边际提升也能转化为显著经济回报。

Method: 使用约300只美股大盘股11年日度数据，训练5类模型，采用贝叶斯优化针对三种统计上不同的市场状态配置超参数以追求交易表现；通过秩聚合（rank aggregation）结合XGBoost和TabNet构建混合集成模型；评估信号精度、噪声鲁棒性、CAPM alpha等指标。

Result: 混合集成模型年化收益51.26%，夏普比率2.44，CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011），beta接近零表明超额收益来自选股；替代数据在技术和基本面特征基础上作用次要，且在空头侧贡献更强；信号精度在测试期四个季度均高于随机基线，组合性能在模拟输入噪声下缓慢下降直至超过阈值后崩溃。

Conclusion: 针对市场状态稳健性的超参数选择有助于提升模型外样本泛化能力；混合集成模型显著优于单一模型；替代数据作用有限且因模型类别而异；该框架具备实时交互探索能力，接入实时数据后即可实际部署。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [387] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有向图学习的谱认证、灵敏度分析和控制方法，基于双侧锥瑞利框架，无需对称性或非负性假设，通过可计算的锥界和可微代理函数实现谱级控制，并在有向Cora数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习导致非对称传播算子，传统谱方法依赖对称性或非负性假设，缺乏针对有向图的谱认证与控制工具，需开发无需这些假设的学习导向方法。

Method: 基于广义铅笔的双侧锥瑞利框架，开发可计算的锥上下界和光滑软最小/最大代理函数，保持严格单侧界并可微；利用左右模态的导数公式进行一阶最优图干预和自适应谱控制。

Result: 数值实验表明方法适用于非锥保持算子和有向学习场景；符号非对称扰动揭示从内部特征对到边界互补准对的转变；在Cora数据集上，自适应重计算在0.5%边权减少预算下降低21.5%谱级，测试准确率不变。

Conclusion: 该方法为有向图学习提供了有效的谱认证与控制工具，证明边方向携带预测信息，对称化可能丢失信息，自适应谱控制可在不损害性能下优化图结构。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [388] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于RSS的新型定位方法，通过最少测量次数和概率建模推断AoA，结合高斯过程和双重随机变分推断重建移动路径，在低功耗下实现了对超轻量接收器（38mg）的高精度追踪（约15m至10m精度），并成功应用于追踪熊蜂归巢飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低功耗定位方法依赖大量RSS测量来推断AoA，受限于范围和功耗，难以适用于超轻量、低功耗设备（如昆虫追踪），需要一种更高效的解决方案。

Method: 使用简单旋转高增益发射器进行最少RSS测量，通过概率建模推断AoA；利用高斯过程建模接收器运动路径，并采用双重随机变分推断进行路径重建。

Result: 在300m范围内，实现了对38mg接收器的追踪，功耗低于180uW时精度约15m，功耗低于600uW时精度提升至约10m；成功应用于熊蜂（Bombus terrestris）归巢飞行追踪。

Conclusion: 该方法为运动生态学、物联网和机器人等领域提供了高效、低功耗的定位解决方案，特别适用于飞行昆虫行为研究等应用场景。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [389] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了规划扩散策略优化（PDPO），一种用于人群导航的离线到在线强化学习框架，通过生成短视界动作块来提升决策多样性，并修正了基准测试中存在的边界评估缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在密集、动态的人群导航中通常仅输出单步反应动作，限制了其表达多样化短期避障策略的能力；同时，常见基准测试缺乏显式边界约束，导致智能体可能通过离开有效区域来规避人群，产生评估假象。

Method: 提出PDPO框架，利用扩散策略生成短视界动作块。该方法首先在避障演示数据上进行预训练，随后将去噪过程视为内部决策过程，使用PPO算法进行在线微调。执行时，策略生成五步动作块并以重ceding-horizon（滚动时域）方式应用。此外，引入将边界违规视为碰撞的新评估设定以修正基准缺陷。

Result: 实验表明，PDPO在成功率上优于强基线方法。消融实验证明，在修正后的有界基准测试中，动作块（action chunks）的生成对性能提升尤为关键。

Conclusion: PDPO通过结合扩散策略的动作块生成能力与在线强化学习微调，有效解决了人群导航中的多模态决策问题，并通过修正评估基准确保了算法在真实约束下的鲁棒性与安全性。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [390] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraceBench框架，用于在受控条件下系统评估LLM代理在时间序列异常检测和根因分析任务中的表现，发现领域上下文能显著提升性能，且代理更倾向于通过数值输出而非可视化来探索数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM代理越来越多地应用于现实系统的时间序列异常检测和根因分析，但在受控条件下对其性能缺乏系统性的评估。

Method: 引入基于模拟的框架TraceBench，通过模拟物理动态系统生成受控的根因归因任务。在该任务中，代理接收时间序列观测数据，需判断系统参数是否被改变并识别具体参数。研究利用三个可解释的机械系统生成任务，并系统评估了四个LLM代理在不同实验条件下的表现。

Result: 1. 领域上下文能显著提升代理的分析能力；2. 代理主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化来探索数据；3. 当要求代理生成将时间序列样本映射到根因标签的Python脚本时，其表现通常不如直接提交预测结果。

Conclusion: TraceBench揭示了LLM代理分析动态系统时间序列数据的机制与局限，相关数据集、代理轨迹、实验结果及排行榜已公开，为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks.
