In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [391] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态网络双重稳健估计器（DynaNet-DR），用于在交互图演化的情况下估计同伴效应，通过控制对比框架索引潜在结果，并在半合成基准测试中展示了良好的估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互图演化的情况下，同伴效应难以估计，因为预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和分配后网络变化具有不同的因果作用，需要一种能够处理这些复杂动态的方法。

Method: 引入控制对比框架，索引潜在结果，开发DynaNet-DR估计器，结合时间因子化倾向和标准化增强，添加代表性分数预测、固定裁剪和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在固定真实时间图序列的半合成基准测试中，DynaNet-DR在针对完整配置文件的方法中显示出有利的估计准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过评估摘要索引对比而非反事实边缘生成，为动态网络中的同伴效应估计提供了一种有效工具，并在MathOverflow研究中进行了观察性说明。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [392] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化支持向量机（SVM）的 Fisher 一致性条件，证明了已知的必要条件（任务损失为度量且每个输出三元组有共同测地线点）对坐标式 argmax 解码器并不充分，并给出了反例和完全分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化 SVM 的一致性理论中，已知的必要条件是否足以保证 argmax 解码器的 Fisher 一致性尚不明确，需要验证该条件的充分性并探索解码器差距。

Method: 通过构造反例（如四输出单位星、K_{2,3} 图、三维 Hamming 立方体）和分析树度量结构，结合原始 - 对偶证书技术，系统分类了满足共同测地线条件的度量空间。

Result: 1. 四输出单位星存在严格非 Bayes 的最优得分向量；2. 树度量中 argmax 一致性当且仅当树为路径；3. 分支树的失败仅限于边界分布；4. 全支撑反例需至少五输出（K_{2,3} 为最小成员）；5. 三维 Hamming 立方体存在全支撑反例。

Conclusion: 共同测地线条件对 argmax 解码器不充分，存在具体解码器差距：嵌入可保证校准链接存在，但不能验证每个代理风险最小化器上的指定 argmax 链接。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [393] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究指出，机器学习在冬小麦氮肥推荐中不应作为标准建议的替代品，而应作为修正步骤；直接以利润损失为评估指标时，纯机器学习模型表现不佳，但结合标准建议并应用简单的阻尼修正后，可显著减少利润损失并消除偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的英国标准氮肥建议未考虑价格波动，而近期价格剧烈波动导致最盈利施氮量发生显著变化；虽然机器学习常被提议为解决手段，但传统仅基于预测准确率的评估无法保证实际利润最大化。

Method: 利用英国两项长期实验中的892条实测产量响应曲线构建测试平台，通过扫描氮肥与谷物价格比率覆盖所有价格情景；直接以“放弃的利润”（profit forgone）对氮肥建议进行评分，评估纯机器学习模型、标准建议及混合修正策略的表现。

Result: 在测试平台上，机器学习作为预测器失效，无一模型能在农场容差内恢复最佳施氮量，且在正常价格下利润表现均不及标准建议；然而，在模型输出后应用简单的修正步骤可减少25%的利润损失，该修正泛化至另一站点可减少43%损失；“标准建议+阻尼修正”的混合策略消除了偏差并减少了罕见的大额损失。

Conclusion: 机器学习的价值在于作为对标准建议的“利润评分修正”而非替代品；这种混合方法不仅优化了经济收益，其价格扫描结果还表明减排成本与当前碳价相当。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [394] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HALO，一种针对惯性测量单元（IMU）的领域专用基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架解决了传感异质性和开放集识别难题，在少参数量下显著优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人类活动识别（HAR）领域缺乏能跨主体、设备和活动泛化的统一模型，主要受限于传感异质性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异）以及对未见活动和标签词汇的泛化能力差。

Method: 采用两阶段训练框架：阶段1利用异质性感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包含自适应池令牌化、通道独立特征提取和上下文传感器条件化；阶段2通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，实现基于余弦相似度检索的开放集识别。

Result: 在10个公开HAR数据集上训练，7个保留数据集上评估，HALO在8个聚合指标上均优于5个最先进基线模型；参数量仅约35M（为MOMENT模型的1/10），但在87个训练标签上的零样本开放集准确率提升了13.7个百分点。

Conclusion: HALO有效解决了IMU数据中的异质性和开放集识别挑战，证明了轻量级领域专用基础模型的优越性，但在极端分布偏移场景下所有模型（包括HALO）仍面临失效问题。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [395] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将重要性评分指标应用于表格数据的多头Transformer模型，实验表明基于该评分移除低重要性头可保持模型鲁棒性，且重要头在各层分布不均，该指标有助于提升架构效率并减少冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型计算成本高且缺乏可解释性，虽然Transformer在视觉和NLP领域研究广泛，但在表格数据领域的应用及可解释性分析仍显不足，亟需一种方法来理解和优化此类模型。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分指标（importance-scoring metric）用于解释多头Transformer模型，并在40个多样化的表格数据集上进行实验，通过逐步移除不同重要性评分的注意力头（attention heads）来评估模型性能变化。

Result: 在72.5%的实验案例中，逐步移除低重要性评分的头时模型性能最稳健；反之，移除最高重要性头会导致分类性能最大幅下降。重要头分散于六个注意力层中，无特定层趋势，且其重要性在不同表格数据集间差异显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的重要性评分指标能有效揭示表格数据Transformer模型的内部机制，通过识别冗余头提升架构效率，相关源代码已公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [396] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“电路压缩”（Circuit Condensation）的方法，通过后训练模型将行为集中到更小的因果图中，显著减少了电路的规模，同时保留了任务性能和通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性方法在发现电路时通常会返回数百条边，导致难以进行彻底检查、比较或验证。

Method: 通过多轮迭代，剪枝低归因边并训练低秩适配器以匹配原始模型，仅当任务性能和通用能力保留时才保留剪枝结果。

Result: 在4种行为和8个模型的32个设置中，有30个设置的压缩电路比最强的冻结基线更小，平均缩小8.1倍，最大可达316倍；重复搜索但不更新权重会导致更大的电路，证明权重更新是减少电路规模的关键。

Conclusion: 电路压缩方法能够有效隔离关键组件，揭示边之间的依赖关系，并生成能够跟踪原始模型下一标记分布并预测其错误的子电路。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [397] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LEON的潜在演化算子网络，用于改进世界动作模型（WAMs）中的潜在状态转移预测，通过基于算子的传播和加法强制机制，在保持控制相关信息的同时提升机器人策略的闭环性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）通常在潜在表示空间中进行预测，以避免完整的外观级生成，但常用的基于Transformer的预测器侧重于token交互而非时间演化，导致在建模潜在状态转移时缺乏针对演化过程的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出了潜在演化算子网络（LEON），基于受控Koopman生成器视角，通过学习可观测空间中的算子传播和加法强制机制来建模潜在演化，将上下文相关的转移变化组织在共享的演化算子结构周围，同时保留加法变化的互补路径。

Result: 在两种不同的WAM formulation中，LEON通过演化特定的归纳偏置和算子传播与强制的互补作用，提升了闭环性能和鲁棒性，并且在完全转移替换下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 转移实现是潜在WAMs中一个重要的架构选择，LEON通过专门的演化建模机制证明了其在提升机器人策略性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [398] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-Spectrum，一种专为多模态光谱到结构解析设计的稀疏混合专家（MoE）框架，通过显式的模态感知路由机制和异构专家容量，解决了多光谱信号异质性导致的性能下降问题，在各种模态设置下均实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从多模态光谱测量中推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异质的信号。然而，直接拼接多光谱序列的常见范式会因显著的异质性和由此产生的多模态不平衡而导致异常的性能下降。

Method: 提出MM-Spectrum框架，引入显式的模态感知路由机制（向路由器暴露光谱身份及token内容表示），并整合共享专家、交互专家及异构专家容量，以提取模态独有信息和跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在全模态、双模态及缺失模态的分子结构解析设置中，MM-Spectrum均取得了一致且显著的性能提升，并通过消融实验和可解释性分析得到了验证。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum通过针对性的架构设计有效克服了多光谱不平衡挑战，为多模态光谱到结构的解析提供了更鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [399] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合经典-量子架构QuantumBoostNet，用于心脏超声视图识别，在噪声环境下表现优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声中准确识别视图或角度对临床诊断至关重要，但现有计算机视觉模型在医学成像任务中因数据噪声高而表现不佳。

Method: QuantumBoostNet采用混合架构，包含经典骨干网络和两个头（经典头和量子头），量子头由参数化10量子比特电路实现，训练分两阶段并通过混合参数自适应切换。

Result: 在心脏超声视图识别任务中，QuantumBoostNet优于现有经典和混合模型，同时在标准图像分类基准上表现优异且对噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 混合经典-量子模型在专业医学成像应用中具有发展潜力，值得继续研究。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [400] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）在大语言模型推理后训练中的机制，发现ES相比GRPO能提供更广的推理覆盖率和更高的Pass@K性能，且参数更新具有功能稀疏性，不会导致灾难性遗忘，并提出了一种结合GRPO和ES优势的序列训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管进化策略（ES）作为一种内存高效的后训练范式在LLM推理中崭露头角，但其优化行为尚未经过系统研究，导致难以明确其相对于主流范式（如GRPO）的优势范围和应用场景。

Method: 通过理论分析（验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性与Pass@K的关系）和实证研究（对比ES与GRPO的熵变化、Pass@1及Pass@K表现），分析ES的动态机制；进一步通过参数漂移分析和功能稀疏性研究，评估ES对模型功能的影响；最后通过超参数实验探究种群大小与模型规模的关系，并提出GRPO-ES序列训练策略。

Result: 1. 理论上证明ES种群多样性有助于提升Pass@K性能；实证显示ES在提升Pass@1的同时，Pass@K显著优于出现熵坍缩的GRPO。2. 发现ES虽引起显著的整模型参数漂移，但性能提升仅源于稀疏的大幅度参数更新子集，且未导致灾难性遗忘。3. 证实ES在更大规模LLM中所需种群规模更小。4. 提出的GRPO-ES序列策略有效结合了两者优势。

Conclusion: ES并非仅仅是GRPO的低效内存替代方案，而是一种具有独特机制的推理后训练范式，其通过维持种群多样性实现更广的推理覆盖，且参数更新具有功能稀疏性，为LLM推理能力的挖掘提供了新路径。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [401] [Group Isomorphism and the Polylogarithmic-Time Hierarchy: Depth-2$\frac{1}{2}$ Circuits and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26257)
*Joshua A. Grochow,Gülce Kardeş,Michael Levet*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘法表模型下群同构问题的低深度电路复杂性，证明了深度2布尔电路需要准多项式规模的下界，并给出了深度2.5均匀电路的准多项式规模上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1970-2025年间的研究逐步将电路深度从多项式降至3.5，但都依赖生成元枚举策略且适用于更一般的拟群。本文旨在探索利用群特有结构的新策略，以进一步降低电路深度。

Method: 提出一种基于群特有结构的新策略：猜测每个群的合成序列及其项的生成元和合成因子的同构类型，然后归纳验证两个合成序列各层对应的扩展是否兼容。该方法结合了短表示猜想、群扩展算法理论和上同调理论。

Result: 证明了深度2布尔电路判定群同构需要准多项式规模的下界；构造了深度2.5的均匀电路，规模为准多项式。

Conclusion: 通过利用群特有的合成序列结构，而非通用的生成元枚举策略，成功将电路深度从3.5降至2.5，为群同构问题的电路复杂性研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the low-depth circuit complexity of Group Isomorphism in the multiplication (Cayley) table model. We prove the first circuit lower bounds for Group Isomorphism: namely, we show that every family of depth-$2$ Boolean circuits deciding Group Isomorphism requires quasipolynomial-size. We complement this with upper bounds of uniform depth-$2\frac{1}{2}$ circuits of quasipolynomial-size.
  A sequence of previous results from 1970-2025 progressively reduced the circuit depth from polynomial to $3\frac{1}{2}$; all of these results relied on the generator-enumerator strategy and, in fact, applied more generally to quasigroups. In contrast, our depth-$2\frac{1}{2}$ construction follows a fundamentally different strategy that exploits structure more specific to groups. We guess a composition series for each group, together with generators for its terms and the isomorphism types of its composition factors. We then inductively verify that the corresponding extensions at each level of the two composition series are compatible. A central part in this approach brings to bear the extensive work on the Short Presentation Conjecture, in tandem with the algorithmic theory of group extensions and cohomology.

</details>


### [402] [Pseudodeterminism and MA != NP^BPP in Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26425)
*Thomas Watson*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文证明了部分布尔函数的零错误随机通信复杂度与双边错误伪确定性通信复杂度之间存在指数级分离，改进了 Göös 等人（STOC 2026）的结果，并将技术推广至分离通信复杂度版本的 MA 与 NP^BPP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进并简化 Göös 等人（STOC 2026）关于随机通信复杂度与伪确定性通信复杂度分离的证明，同时扩展技术以分离通信复杂度类比中的 MA 与 NP^BPP 类。

Method: 通过构造特定的部分布尔函数，证明零错误随机通信复杂度与双边错误伪确定性通信复杂度之间的指数级分离，并推广该技术至更复杂的复杂度类分离。

Result: 实现了零错误随机与双边错误伪确定性通信复杂度的指数级分离，简化了先前证明，并成功分离了通信复杂度版本的 MA 与 NP^BPP。

Conclusion: 该研究在通信复杂度理论中取得了重要进展，为理解不同错误模型下的计算复杂度提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We prove an exponential separation between zero-sided-error randomized and two-sided-error pseudodeterministic communication complexities of partial boolean functions. This qualitatively improves and simplifies the proof of the separation by Göös, Harms, Riazanov, Sofronova, Sokolov, and Yuan (STOC 2026), which had a two-sided-error randomized upper bound. Then, we generalize our technique to separate the communication complexity analogues of MA and NP^BPP.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [403] [Buy-at-Bulk Facility Location on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26337)
*Shamisa Nematollahi,Daniel Vaz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了买批量设施定位问题（BBFL）及其变体kCFL在树形实例上的近似算法，针对单位需求和可拆分需求提出了PTAS，并针对电缆不可拆分需求证明了近似下界并给出了资源增广下的最优解算法及2-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题结合了经典设施定位与买批量网络设计，源于电信网络中的实际应用场景，旨在最小化设施开启成本与基于容量成本函数的边传输成本之和。

Method: 针对树形拓扑结构，分别分析了单位需求/可拆分变体和电缆不可拆分变体（kCFL）。对于前者设计了多项式时间近似方案（PTAS）；对于后者，首先在星型图上证明了优于3/2的近似是NP难的，随后提出了一种在树实例上允许每条电缆容量超额(1+ε)倍以获得最优成本的资源增广算法。

Result: 1. 单位需求和可拆分BBFL在树上存在PTAS；2. 电缆不可拆分kCFL在星型图上难以近似到3/2以内；3. 提出了树实例上的资源增广算法（容量超额1+ε，成本最优）；4. 导出了树实例上kCFL的2-近似算法。

Conclusion: 在树形网络结构中，买批量设施定位问题及其电缆不可拆分变体具有良好的近似性质，通过资源增广技术可有效解决NP难的容量约束问题，实现了从理论下界到实用近似算法的突破。

Abstract: We consider the buy-at-bulk facility location problem (BBFL), a problem combining the classic facility location problem with buy-at-bulk network design, which finds motivation in telecommunication networks. In it, we are given a graph with edge lengths, opening costs and demands for each vertex, and a monotone and subadditive capacity-cost function, and our task is to open facilities on a subset of the vertices and route the demand from each vertex to these facilities. The cost of a solution (which we want to minimize) is given by the opening costs of the chosen facilities, plus the cost on each edge, which is given by its length times the cost of providing enough capacity for the demands through the edge, given by the capacity-cost function. A common variant of the problem, the $k$-cable facility location problem (kCFL), considers the case where capacity is provided by buying copies of given cable types, each with a certain capacity and cost.
  We study BBFL on tree instances and show, for the unit-demand and splittable variants, that the problem admits a PTAS (a $(1+ε)$-approximation for any $ε> 0$). We also consider kCFL in the new setting of cable-unsplittable demands, where the demand of a vertex cannot be split among multiple cables. We show that the problem is NP-hard to approximate to a factor better than $3/2$ on stars, and then provide an algorithm for tree instances that outputs a solution with optimal cost, but which exceeds the capacity on each cable by a factor of $1+ε$. As a consequence, we show that the problem has a $2$-approximation algorithm on trees.

</details>


### [404] [The Time-Dependent Traveling Salesman Problem with Loose Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26360)
*Francisco J. Soulignac*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解带时间窗的时变旅行商问题（TDTSPTW）的精确框架，通过结合动态规划标记算法、列生成、ng-memory 增强和精确搜索，有效解决了多达 45 个客户的实例，并在宽松时间窗下扩展了可求解范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的带时间窗旅行商问题（TSPTW）未考虑拥堵对行程时间的影响，而 TDTSPTW 通过引入时变因素更贴近现实，但求解难度更大，需要更高效的精确算法来应对不同时间窗松紧程度的实例。

Method: 采用基于动态规划的标记算法，结合列生成、ng-memory 增强和精确搜索技术，利用完成界限进行状态空间稀疏化、变量固定和精确搜索剪枝，并嵌入分支定价（branch-and-price）方法中。

Result: 在包含超过 10,000 个实例的基准测试中，该框架成功求解了所有最多 45 个客户的实例，包括所有最多 50 个客户的无时间窗实例，且在宽松时间窗下显著扩展了可最优求解的实例范围。

Conclusion: 所提出的精确框架在处理 TDTSPTW 问题上表现出高效性和鲁棒性，适用于不同时间窗紧度水平的实例，为时变路径优化问题提供了有效的求解工具。

Abstract: The time-dependent traveling salesman problem with time windows (TDTSPTW) generalizes the well-known traveling salesman problem with time windows by accounting the effects of congestion on travel times. In this paper, we develop an exact framework for the TDTSPTW with a makespan objective that extends the range of instances solvable to optimality under loose time windows while remaining effective across all levels of time-window tightness. Our framework relies on a dynamic-programming labeling algorithm and combines column generation, ng-memory augmentation, and exact search, using completion bounds for state-space sparsification, variable fixing, and exact search pruning. Embedded within a branch-and-price method, the framework solves all instances with up to 45 customers in a benchmark comprising more than 10,000 instances, including all instances without time windows with up to 50 customers.

</details>


### [405] [Unpublished Draft: A Post-Processing Approach to Fairness in Tie-Aware Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26478)
*Somya Nigam,Johan Springael,Kenneth Sörensen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对公平排名聚合中普遍存在的“并列（ties）”问题，提出了一种后处理方法，可将不公平的并列感知共识排名转化为最接近的公平共识排名，并提供了精确算法和快速启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平排名聚合研究主要假设输入和输出均为严格排序的排列（permutations），忽略了实践中更常见的包含并列（ties）的排名情况，导致理论与实际应用之间存在差距。

Method: 提出了一种后处理（post-processing）方法，包含一个用于寻找最接近公平共识排名的精确算法（exact algorithm）和一个快速启发式算法（fast heuristic）。

Result: 实现了在给定不公平的并列感知共识排名时，能够高效地确定最接近的公平共识排名。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了公平排名聚合领域在处理并列排名时的空白，为实际应用提供了有效的算法支持。

Abstract: The problem of finding a fair consensus ranking is an active research topic in the domain of fair rank aggregation and has been well studied; however, existing studies predominantly consider both the input rankings and the output ranking to be permutations where elements are always strictly ordered. In practice, however, a ranking with ties is far more common. This study bridges this gap by presenting a post-processing approach to determine the closest fair consensus ranking when an unfair tie-aware consensus ranking is provided. It proposes an exact algorithm and a fast heuristic to achieve this.

</details>


### [406] [Cheaper by the Batch: Shared Traversal for Genotype Graph Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26488)
*Aaron Li,Yifan Li,Drew DeHaas,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种批量突变重映射算法，通过单次共享逆拓扑遍历替代独立遍历，在保持精确载体集语义的同时，比独立重映射快10.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体遗传学中，基因型表示图（GRG）需要频繁更新节点以保持语义并复用现有结构，现有方法逐个重映射突变效率低下。

Method: 引入批量突变重映射算法，采用单次共享逆拓扑遍历，传播紧凑的位并行每突变状态，并使用适应性的稀疏/密集载体集表示。

Result: 在受控更新工作负载和端到端等位基因极化测试中，该方法比独立重映射快10.5倍，同时保持精确的载体集语义。

Conclusion: 批量处理是内存可扩展的并行化补充方案，能高效处理群体遗传分析中的批量载体集更新问题。

Abstract: Updating a graph by inserting or replacing nodes while preserving semantics and reusing existing structure is a recurring computational problem. In population genetics, this problem arises in the genotype representation graph (GRG), a directed acyclic graph that losslessly encodes phased genetic variation across hundreds of thousands of samples by sharing subgraph structure for individual mutations. In a GRG, each mutation's carrier set is implicitly encoded as the set of leaf nodes reachable from the node it is assigned to. Updating a mutation is therefore a structural editing problem, and current approaches remap mutations individually.
  This paper introduces a batched mutation-remapping algorithm that replaces independent reuse-aware traversals with a single shared reverse-topological pass, identifying reuse candidates for an entire batch at once. The pass propagates compact bit-parallel per-mutation state and uses an adaptive sparse/dense carrier set representation spanning rare-to-common variant densities. Batching is the memory-scalable complement to split-based parallelism, which instead replicates graph and traversal state per worker. Our remapping is evaluated on a controlled update workload and on end-to-end allele polarization, a bulk carrier set update that is common in population genetic analysis. Our approach is up to 10.5$\times$ faster than independent remapping while preserving exact carrier-set semantics.

</details>


### [407] [Hadamard Flattening and Gaussian Pooling Sketch for Least Squares with Coordinate-wise Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26552)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的快速稠密随机变换方法，用于加速过约束的$\ell_2$回归问题，实现了$\ell_\infty$范数下的坐标级精度保证，所需行数为$O(\epsilon^{-2}d\log d)$，计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机草图求解算法在$\ell_2$回归中虽能保持回归成本，但在$\ell_\infty$范数下保证解向量的坐标级精度较为困难。之前的研究（如Song等人）声称改进了行数，但其证明依赖不成立的独立性假设。

Method: 引入一种新的快速稠密随机变换，结合随机Hadamard扁平化、随机置换和平衡的不相交高斯池化。通过Hadamard和置换阶段后的条件独立性，使草图问题成为精确的高斯回归。

Result: 实现了$m=O(\epsilon^{-2}d\log d)$行数的$\ell_\infty$保证，使用一次Hadamard传递，填充内部维度$N=\widetilde{O}(n+\epsilon^{-2}d^3)$，计算时间为$\widetilde{O}(nd+\epsilon^{-2}d^4)$。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了之前工作中独立性假设不成立的问题，真正实现了接近$d$线性行数的$\ell_\infty$精度保证，且计算高效。

Abstract: Randomized sketch-and-solve algorithms accelerate overconstrained $\ell_2$ regression by replacing the input with a smaller problem. Standard subspace embeddings guarantee that the cost of the regression is nearly preserved, but coordinate-wise accuracy of the solution is more delicate: we want the solution vector itself to be close to the optimal solution in $\ell_\infty$ norm. In particular, we want to find a vector $x'\in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $\|x'-x^*\|_\infty\leq \fracε{\sqrt d}\cdot \|Ax^\star-b\|_2\cdot \|A^\dagger\|_{\rm op}$. Price, Song and Woodruff initiated the study of this problem and showed that the subsampled randomized Hadamard transform (SRHT) with $O(ε^{-2} d^{1+Θ(\sqrt{\log\log n/\log d})})$ rows achieves this guarantee. A subsequent work of Song, Ye, Yin and Zhang claimed to improve the row count to $O(ε^{-2}d\log^3 n)$. Unfortunately, their proof relies on an independence assumption that does not hold in general, and we exhibit an explicit instance on which it fails.
  To achieve a truly nearly-linear-in-$d$ row count, we introduce a new fast, dense randomized transform, which combines a randomized Hadamard flattening, a random permutation, and balanced, disjoint Gaussian pooling. Conditioned on the Hadamard-and-permutation stage, the sketched problem becomes an exact Gaussian regression in which the noise is independent of the entire sketched design; this conditional independence is exactly what the earlier argument was missing. Our sketch yields the $\ell_\infty$ guarantee with $m=O(ε^{-2}d\log d)$ rows, uses one Hadamard pass with a padded internal dimension $N=\widetilde{O}(n+ε^{-2}d^3)$, and is efficient to apply: the sketched pair $(SA, Sb)$ can be computed in $O(Nd\log N)=\widetilde{O}(nd+ε^{-2}d^4)$ time.

</details>


### [408] [On the Instance Optimality of Bidirectional Dijkstra's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26952)
*Matic Požar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文修正了此前关于Dijkstra算法实例最优性分析的缺陷，提出了一种改进的双向Dijkstra算法变体，证明了其在加权图中的实例最优性，并在无权图中确立了$O(Δ)$的下界，同时在特定条件下解决了简单图的实例最优性开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Haeupler等人关于最短路径算法实例最优性研究的启发，旨在重新审视标准查询模型下最短$st$-path算法的实例最优性问题，并解决现有分析中存在的缺陷。

Method: 1. 构造反例以指出单向和双向Dijkstra算法在实例最优性分析中的问题；2. 提出双向Dijkstra算法的最小简化修改方案；3. 提供无权图下界证明的简化版本；4. 针对满足特定节点/边比例关系或最大度数约束的简单图实例进行理论推导。

Result: 1. 证明了改进后的双向Dijkstra变体在加权设置下具有实例最优性；2. 确认无权图中不存在优于$O(Δ)$因子（$Δ$为最大度数）的实例最优算法；3. 在$n \ge m/16$或最大度数不超过探索边数平方根的广泛实例类中，实现了常数因子内的实例最优性。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅纠正了先前理论的错误，还通过算法改进和严格的界限分析，显著推进了最短路径算法在加权、无权及特定简单图场景下的实例最优性理论，为近似算法的设计提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Recent work by Haeupler, Hladík, Rozhon, Tarjan, and Tětek on the instance optimality of shortest-path algorithms established several results concerning Dijkstra's algorithm and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithm in weighted and unweighted graphs. Motivated by these results, we revisit the question of instance optimality for shortest $st$-path algorithms in the standard query model.
  We identify several issues in the analysis of the instance optimality of both unidirectional and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithms and provide corresponding counterexamples. We then propose a minimal simple modification of the bidirectional Dijkstra algorithm and prove that the resulting variant is instance optimal in the weighted setting. Furthermore, we revisit the unweighted case, provide a simplified proof of the lower bound showing that no algorithm can achieve instance optimality up to a factor better than $O(Δ)$, where $Δ$ denotes the maximum degree of the graph, and discuss the implications of this result for approximation algorithms. Finally, we make progress on the open problem of instance optimality in simple graphs. We show that if the problem instance satisfies $n\ge m/16$, where $n$ is the number of nodes and $m$ is the number of edges queried by our algorithm, then it is optimal up to a constant factor. Additionally, we show instance optimality for a broad class of instances, in particular when the largest degree in the graph is at most the square root of the number of explored edges, our algorithm exhibits optimality up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [409] [The Randomized Query Complexity of Finding Minimal Elements in Bounded-Width Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26981)
*Luyao Fan,Jiayang Zou,Jiayang Gao,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在宽度至多为w的n元素偏序集中查找所有最小元素的零错误随机查询复杂度，证明了有限下界，表明已知的随机上界具有正确的渐近主导常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作建立了随机上界，但相应的下界在主导常数中存在乘法差距，随着w的增长，该差距接近因子2。本文旨在解决这一差距，确定正确的渐近主导常数。

Method: 基于随机链硬分布下不可比查询的成对计数，使用组件翻转对合和不可比比较的唯一所有权性质进行论证。

Result: 证明了有限下界 \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w}\ge \frac{w+1}{2}n-\frac{w(w+3)}4 +w\left(1-\frac1w\right)^n +\frac{w(w-1)}4\left(1-\frac2w\right)^n \)，从而得出对于每个固定的w，\( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w} =\left(\frac{w+1}{2}+o(1)\right)n \)。

Conclusion: 已知的随机上界对于每个固定宽度具有正确的渐近主导常数，解决了先前工作中存在的乘法差距问题。

Abstract: We study the zero-error randomized query complexity of finding all minimal elements in an unknown $n$-element poset of width at most $w$. Previous work of Daskalakis, Karp, Mossel, Riesenfeld, and Verbin established a randomized upper bound with leading term $\frac{w+1}{2}n$, while the corresponding lower bound left a multiplicative gap in the leading constant that approaches a factor of 2 as $w$ grows. We prove the finite lower bound \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w}\ge \frac{w+1}{2}n-\frac{w(w+3)}4 +w\left(1-\frac1w\right)^n +\frac{w(w-1)}4\left(1-\frac2w\right)^n. \) Consequently, for every fixed $w$, \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w} =
\left(\frac{w+1}{2}+o(1)\right)n. \) Thus the known randomized upper bound has the correct asymptotic leading constant for every fixed width. The argument is based on a pairwise accounting of incomparable queries under a random-chain hard distribution, using a component-flip involution and a unique ownership property for incomparable comparisons. Generative AI was used in the preparation of this manuscript.

</details>


### [410] [Product Structure Meets Track Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27096)
*Michael A. Bekos,Giordano Da Lozzo,Petr Hliněný,Michael Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算法，用于计算具有乘积结构图的轨道布局（track layout）上界，为平面图、1-平面图及多种$k$-相关图类建立了新的或匹配最优的轨道数上界，且算法具有线性时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定图的轨道数（即图 admits 轨道布局所需的最小轨道数），特别是针对具有特定乘积结构（product structure）的图类，填补现有文献中关于此类图轨道数上界的空白或优化现有界限。

Method: 提出一种算法，计算强乘积 $P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$ 的子图的轨道布局。该算法利用初等链表数据结构，基于路径幂 $P^h$、完全图 $K_r$ 及图 $H$ 的结构特性，构建至多 $(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$ 个轨道的布局。

Result: 1. 平面图轨道数上界匹配当前最优值 225；
2. 1-平面图和最优 2-平面图轨道数上界为 375；
3. 为 genus-$k$、$k$-平面图、$k$-framed、$k$-map 及 $k$-string 图类建立了仅依赖于 $k$ 的新上界；
4. 算法在提供乘积结构分解的情况下，时间复杂度为 $O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$，对平面图呈线性时间。

Conclusion: 该算法通过结合图的乘积结构理论，有效解决了多类重要图族的轨道数上界问题，不仅复现了平面图的最佳已知结果，还显著扩展了适用范围至更复杂的图类，并保证了高效的计算性能。

Abstract: A track layout of a graph is a partition of its vertices into linearly ordered independent sets, called tracks, such that no two edges between the same pair of tracks cross. Given a graph, the goal in this context is to determine its track number, that is, the minimum number of tracks required for the graph to admit a track layout.
  In this work, we present upper bounds on the track number of graphs admitting a product structure. Our main contribution is an algorithm that computes a track layout with at most $(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$ tracks for every subgraph of the strong product $P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$, where $P^h$ is the $h$-th power of a path $P$, $K_r$ is the complete graph on $r$ vertices, and $H$ is a graph with track number $tn(H)$. Combined with existing product-structure results from the literature, this algorithm yields upper bounds on the track number of several graph classes. For planar graphs, the obtained bound matches the current best-known upper bound of $225$. For $1$-planar and optimal $2$-planar graphs, our algorithm yields track layouts with at most $375$ tracks, while for genus-$k$, $k$-planar, $k$-framed, $k$-map, and $k$-string graphs it provides track layouts with a number of tracks that depends solely on $k$, thus establishing new upper bounds on the track number for these graph classes. The algorithm runs in linear time for planar graphs and, more generally, in $O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$ time whenever a corresponding product-structure decomposition of the input $n$-vertex graph is provided as part of the input, where $t=tn(H)$ and $f_t(H)$ is the time needed to compute a $t$-track layout of $H$. Furthermore, our algorithm only uses elementary linked-list data structures.

</details>
